                       tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ varalāṟu
             (vi.kō. cūriyanārāyaṇa cāstiriyār avarkaḷ
             iyaṟṟiya nūṟṟokuti - iraṇṭām pakuti )

             "rūpāvati" allatu "kāṇāmaṟ pōṉa makaḷ"
       Dedication :

   To
       THE HONORABLE JUSTICE
       SIR S. SUBRAHMANYA AIYAR AVL., K.C.I.E.
       THIS DRAMA IS DEDICATED
       as a token of respect and esteem
       By the Author

   --------
   urimaiyurai
   eḻucīrkkaḻi neṭilaṭi yāciriya viruttam.
   colvaḷar maturait toṉṉakar maṇiyē
             tōmilan taṇarkula viḷakkē
       nalvaḷañ cāṉṟa cuppiramaṇiya
             nampiyē niyāyavāṉ matiyē
       kalvicēr rūpā vatiyeṉu nūlaik
             kaḷippuṭaṉ kaiyurai koṭuttu
       molvakai yuṇartan tuṭporu ṇāṭi
             yurimaiyiṟ koḷḷuti yuvantē.

   ----------------

                                INTRODUCTION

   Of the time- honoured trifurcation of Tamil Literature into Verse,
   Music, and the Drama, the last has few or no representatives while the
   first and the second have many. The positive want of ancient dramatic
   literature has been accounted for by the lovers of Tamil in various
   ways, which seem more or less fabulous to those who have any tincture
   of English education. That the Tamil speaking people have included the
   histrionic art among the degraded arts of life, fit to be pursued by
   the wandering nomads, or by the dregs of societies, seems to point to
   the conclusion that the art in due course of time have degenerated and
   fallen into disrepute or lacked cultivation at the hands of men of
   genius. In either case, any attempt I that direction in these days of
   western culture can not but add to the reputation of one who, with
   necessary intellectual equipment, energy, and zeal, devotes his nights
   and days to it.
   In Madras, as in the Mofussil, we often come across travelling troops
   of actors amusing audience with variety of plays based on the three
   great magazines* of Poetic fiction, Ramayana, Bharatam, and
   Kandapuranam. They are for the most parts comical, and abound in vulgar
   conceits and voluptuous songs, gratifying to the vicious, but bearing
   no literary value.
   If any drama has seen the light, that mingles interest with
   instruction, it is the excellent Manonmaniyam, by the late* lamented
   professor Sundaram Pillai, M.A., published a few years ago. Eminently
   classical as it is in diction, metre and matter, it is simply excellent
   in characterisation and plot evolution, and will do well on the boards.
   As a drama, what Manonmaiyam is in verse, Rupavati is in prose. Though
   it is no praise to say that both are free from the Pigeon-Tamil Which
   obtains so much even among the Scholars of Southern India, who however
   imbued with western knowledge and stirred up by Western example from
   their wonted lethargy to a sense of national literature, only pander to
   the tastes of low and the vulgar, our new-fledged writers would do well
   to consult, the choice and chaste direction of these two dramas if they
   would provide for their earnest readers opportunities of knowing what
   the classical Tamil is, and what great purpose it can be made to serve.
   Our Sastriar has in Rupavati pressed into service, whenever necessary,
   the facilities* of Tamil diction, pure and unmixed, and given the
   reading public not only an intellectual feat but so vivid portraiture
   of the ways and manners of princess and people of the age of the
   literary *Witenagement in Madura that their imagination* cannot but be
   quickened, exalted, and *ennobled. If he has succeeded in this as I
   verily believe he has, Rupavati fulfils the mission it was intended to
   fulfil Into tears of joy for, now appeared before them Sundara and
   Surupa stripped of their disguise and clad in their usual apparel.
   Drunk with joy at the sight of their missing son and daughter, they
   made haste to unite the bands of those who were already united in their
   hearts; and Surasena, convinced of the cruelty done to Nayavachana,
   ordered his release and begged his pardon. Then Surasena invited
   Satguna, Virendra and others to his metropolis and wished that a day
   should be fixed for the marriage. An astrologer was consulted and the
   marriage of Ambujakshi, Suchila's daughter, to Sukumara and Kanakamalai
   to Nayavachana's son Chandramukha, were proposed. Thus three marriages
   had tobe celebrated. But three is a number of very bad omen, and so
   they arranged for the celebrations of their marriage on the day,
   Friday, when happily fell the sixtieth-year ceremony of the laureate of
   the Pandian Court, Vidhya Sagara, the guru of Sundarananda and his
   associates. Thus was the objection obviated and the occasion made
   jubiliant all around.
   "O world, thy slippery turns, Fullest foes,
       Whose passions and whose plots have broke their sleep
       To take the one the other, by some chance,
       Some trick not worth on egg, shall grow dear friends,
       And inter joke their issues"

   Rupavathi, the first fruit of the author is excellent in its own way.
   It contains magnificent speeches which would repay perusal and the
   obstacles in it and almost all poetical or have poetry in them.
   Rupavathi, the heroine, is the only daughter of Surasena. Endowed by
   nature with the gifts of body and mind, she falls in love with the only
   son of Satguna. The parents of the lovers live only for their children,
   and but for them their life and dominion are trills "light as air". The
   angelic heroine is a perfect dissembler and is well acquainted with the
   laws of fashion and etiquette. In Act I, Scene 2, her apparently
   innocent questions 'will one swoon when he weaps? To continue the
   narrative, and the skillful way in which she tries to draw out form her
   companions an unvarnished tale of her lover's character and
   disposition, and the sound rebuke administered to Kanakamalai for her
   interference while speaking amply testify to the statement. Further,
   she is a poetic being and often speaks verses; she appreciates music
   and loves songs; she exhibits the power of her tongue in the scene in
   which she brings down Sundarananda to agree to their instance escape;
   she braves all fatigues and hardships, being supported by the strength
   of her love; she keeps a steady reserve in the court of Chola to avoid
   detection. Her part, in this drama reminds us of the charming group of
   Shakespeare's women such as Julia, Viola, Portia, Rosalind and Imogen
   who, under force of circumstances assumed the disguise of male attire,
   What distinguishes Rupavathi most is her learning, intelligence and
   fidelity in love.
   Sundarananda, her lover, is a man of superior intelligence,
   well-educated, and well-versed in the art of war. He is a Dhirodatta,
   to use the expression of the Sanskrit dramatistik, and, in the language
   of Suresena, he is an incarnation of meekness and gentleness, and is a
   poet as well as a songster. His learning is manifest in his explanation
   of the name Thiruvappanar and in his reference to Ahapporul ilakkanam
   (Act III, scene 6). The most remarkable trait in his character, besides
   his love to God, is his great attachment to his parents, of which he
   thinkswith pious affection and tearful eyes, when they are doomed to
   suffer the hardships of exile and when he is himself oppressed by the
   pangs of love. Another noble trait in him is his gratefulness to his
   benefactors.
   Satguna, his father, is an incarnation of virtue. Full of piety and
   calm in judgement, he is loved by his subjects as well as by his
   enemies. His wife, Sundari, is a good match for him in every respect.
   Suchila, his dutiful minister, is a subtle psychologist and sees
   through Surasena. He is convinced that virtue will triumph independent
   of extraneous aid. His ministerial tact is manifest in the scene where
   he negotiates peace between Surasena and Virendra.
   Surasena, father of the heroine, is rough, rude and ravengold. His
   conquest has, as his daughter says, made him amibitious and hardened
   his heart. He would yield to no persuation but force. It was only when
   his forces failed that he conferred with his minister. The only
   redeeming point in his character is that he is capable of remorse, and
   it is probably due to this fact that he is not murdered, though he
   richly deserved it, for his tyranny.
   Jnanadipa is a sage indeed. He is very hospitable to strangers, and his
   learned love is manifested in the scences where his disciple appears.
   Unmindful of worldly honours, he rebukes prince and peasant alike for
   their folly.
   Vidya Sagara, the ocean of learning, is the Guru to the Prince and his
   associates. All rever him for his wisdom, and quote his verses and sign
   his songs most of all , Ambujakshi a clever songstress. The scene in
   which he figures before Surasena and thunders forth his highly
   eulogistic verses commemorating Surasena's late victory followed by his
   learned interpretation thereof, is comical enough, when viewed in the
   light of his question, 'Am I to be beaten?'. But this apparent
   inconsistency illustrates a not uncommon occurrence among human beings,
   viz., that when fear overtakes a man, his learning quits him and his
   faculty of argumentation is at a stand.
   Chandramukha and Sukamara, are the companions of the Prince to whom
   they are firmly attached. The former relies upon experience and human
   effort for success in life while the latter resigns everything to the
   will of Providence. Hence these two characters represent two great
   schools of Philosophy.
   Imbujakshi and Kanakamalai are the two associates of the Princess.
   Former, a clever songstress, is shy by nature whereas Kanakamalai is
   petulmeddlesome. Further, the latter, thogh slightly superstitious,
   often nails on the head when she has to judge of character.
   The Lords appear in three of the scenes; and with Nayavachana,
   Surasena, they lay their heads together to plot his death. The first
   Lord is collected in his procedure and preaches against precipitancy in
   matters moment. The second Lord, rather hasty, is wise on second
   thoughts, and the third is a ruffian ready for immediate action and
   thirsting for revenge.
   Nayavachana isa man of policy. He always veneers his thoughts,f or
   Surasena falls out with him, and he cannot be brow-beaten when he sees
   tyranny prevails. When the Lords are for the assassination of Surasena,
   criticizes his judgement, and calmly refers the question to the
   decision of Sudananda, of whose superior intelligence he has a high
   opinion.
   Three DFreams are introduced in this play, two of them by Surasena,
   Kamalavallai prognosticating evil and the third by the Nurse giving a
   ray of the reappearance of Rupavati.
   The Epilogue is spoken by Sundarananda. In it he discourses on human
   and human love and rightly points out that domestic life, if rightly
   led, is better and productive of better results than the life of an
   ascestic who prefers seclusion and hates mankind.
   To conclude, The plot of Rupavati, is so simple and dexterously
   consistent and characterization so notable and happy, that they comine
   to give the pleasure of the intellectual feasts given in it. I trust
   that the entire play wright will continue to amuse and instruct the
   reading public, year after year,with some fresh creations of his own,
   and will reclaim the dramatic love which is highly educative in its
   value but much neglected by the Tamil people.
   Coimbatore, March 1902 M.S. PURNALINGAM PILLAI.
   --------------------
                     ___________________________________

                                  mukavurai

   cēranāṭṭaracaṉākiya cūracēnavarmaṉ caṟkuṇavaḻuti yeṉṟa pāṇṭiyarācaṉait
   tirupparaṅ kuṉṟattiṟ karukē naṭanta pōriṉ kaṇ veṉṟu maturaiyampatiyait
   taṉakku irācatāṉiyākkik koṇṭu pāṇṭiyaṉaiyum avaṉ maṉaiviyiṉaiyum kāṭṭil
   turattiviṭṭu vaḻutimakaṉ cuntarānantaṉai māttirañciṟaiccālaiyiliṭṭu
   aracupurintu vantāṉ. iṅṅaṉa mirukkaiyil orunāṇmālai, cuntarānantaṉ tāṉ
   veḷiyiṟ pōkum pōtu taṉṉaik kākkumāṟu niyamikkappaṭṭa taṉ ṟōḻarākiya
   cukumāra cantiramukarkaḷōṭu araṇ maṉaik kaṭuttatōr pūṅkāvaṉañceṉṟu
   taṉatu peṟṟōrkkuṟṟa katiyiṉaiyeṇṇi ēṅkiṉāṉ.
   avvamayattu cēraṉ makaḷākiya rūpāvati taṉṟōḻimārākiya ampujāṭci
   kanakamālaikaḷōṭu ulāvic celpavaḷ cuntarānantaṉaik kaṇṇuṟṟuk
   kātalkoṇṭāḷ. iṅṅaṉañ cuntarānantaṉmītu kātal koṇṭa rūpāvati vīṭṭiṟkēkit
   taṉ uḷḷaṅkoḷḷai koṇṭa talaivaṉai niṉaintu niṉaintu uruki āṟṟātu nāṭōṟum
   pūñcōlaikkuc ceṉṟu taṉ kātala ṉait taricittuc ciṟituciṟitu āṟā niṟpāḷ.
   ivvāṟē cuntarānantaṉum rūpāvatiyiṉmītu meynnēcamuṟṟu ayarvāṉāyiṉāṉ.
   itaṟkiṭaiyiṟ caṟkuṇavaḻutiyum avaṉ maṉaiviyum kāṭṭil oru vēṭaṉutaviyāṉ
   ñānatīpa muṉivaratu āciramamaṭaintu muṉivarākkiṉaippaṭi avviṭam viṭṭup
   puṟappaṭṭuccōṇāṭu ceṉṟu, tañcaimānakaril vīrēntira cōḻaṉ araṇmaṉaiyil
   vativārāyiṉār. cūracēnavarmaṉō vaḻutiyait turattiyatu mutaṟ kuṭikaḷiṭam
   koṭiyaṉeṉap pērpeṟṟu veṟukkap paṭṭāṉ. atuvumaṉṟi avaṉukku
   naṇpaṉāyirunta nayavacanaṉuñ ciṟcila kaṉavāṉkaḷōṭu varmaṉait
   tolaikkumvaḻi nāṭuvaṉāyiṉāṉ.
   maṟṟaippaṭi rūpāvati cuntarānantaṉ ākiya ivviruvaruṭaiya kātalum
   uraiyāṭṭamillaiyāyiṉum vaḷarpiṟai pōṉ mutirāniṉṟatu. āyiṉuñ
   cuntarānantaṉ maṭṭum iṭaiyiṭaiyil taṉatu peṟṟōrai yeṇṇi varuntuvāṉ.
   ippaṭiyiruntu varunāḷil, tāṉ kāṭṭil turattiya caṟkuṇavaḻutiyum avaṉ
   maṉaiviyum cōṇāṭṭiliruppatait taṉṉoṟṟaṟāl uṇarnta cūracēkavarmaṉ
   'pāṇṭiyaṉuñ cōḻaṉun taṉmītu paṭaiyeṭuttu varuvārkaḷāyiṉ tāṉ
   ciṟaiyiliṭṭirukkum vaḻutimakaṉ cuntarānantaṉaik koṉṟuviṭuvatu eṉṟu
   taṉakkuḷḷē tīrmāṉittukkoṇṭāṉ. ivvaṇṇam ivaṉ tīrmāṉittatai uṇarnta ivaṉ
   makaḷ rūpāvati taṉ kātalaṉākiya cuntarānantaṉukkut taṉ tantaiyiṉ
   antaraṅka eṇṇattaiyum cuntarānantaṉ taṉṉai vantu maṟunāḷiraviṟ
   kāṇavēṇṭu meṉṉun taṉ viruppattaiyum oru kaṭitattil varaintu piṉṉart tāṉ
   taṉatu nāyakaṉirukkuñ ciṟaiccālaiyiṉ irakaciya vaḻip pūṭṭiṉ
   tiṟavukōlaiyeṭuttu an nirupattiṉkaṇ vaittuc curuṭṭit taṉ talaivaṉatu
   ciṟaiccālaikkuḷ eṟintāḷ. ataṉ piṟaku cuntarānantaṉ kaṭitaṅkaṇṭu ivvāṟu
   ceytavaḷ rūpāvatiyēyeṉṟu teḷintu piṟṟaināḷiravil veḷiyiṟ pōntu taṉ
   talaiviyiṉ āṇaippirakāram avaḷaik kāṇpāṉ viḻaintu niṟpuḻi, rūpāvati
   āṇvēṭam pūṇṭuvantu taṉatu arumait talaivaṉaic cantittu avaṉōṭu vātāṭik
   kaṭaiciyil avaṉai uṭaṉpōkkiṟku iṇakkiyapiṉ, iruvaruñ cērntu maturaimā
   nakaraiviṭṭup puṟappaṭṭu vaikaṟaip pōḻtiṉ ñānatīparatu ācciramamuṟṟuc
   caṟṟiḷaippāṟiya piṉ mīṭṭum puṟappaṭṭuc cōṇāṭaṭaintu cuntara curuṭar
   eṉṉum vēṟṟunāmaṅkaḷōṭu avviṭam iruppārāyiṉār.
   itu niṟka. poḻutu pularntapiṉ ciṟai kāvalāḷar cuntarānantaṉaik kāṇātu
   kavalai kūrntu, piṟaku 'avaṉ ciṟai matilēṟik kutit tōṭiṉaṉ ātaliṉ
   kollappaṭṭāṉ' eṉṟoru poyk katai kaṟpittu varumaṉai nampumāṟu ceytaṉar.
   ataṉpiṉ varumaṉ antappurañ ceṉṟu āṇṭu araṟṟāniṟkum kōmaḷa valli
   cevilikaḷiṉ mūlamāyt taṉmakaḷ rūpāvatiyaik kāṇa villai eṉpatai uṇarntu
   eṅkun tēṭiyum akappaṭāmaiyāl 'innāṭṭārē taṉmakaḷai iravileṭuttuc ceṉṟu
   kolaipurintaṉar' eṉṟu tuṇintu ūriluḷḷāraiyellām oṟukkap pukuntāṉ.
   taṉatu naṇpaṉākiya nayavacanaṉ mītum aiyamuṟṟu avaṉaic
   ciṟaippaṭuttiṉāṉ.
   ivvāṟu varumaṉ ceyyum anitaṅkaḷaip poṟukka māṭṭātu caṉaṅkaḷ varuntā
   niṟpac ciṉṉāṭ kaḻiyalum, cōṇāṭṭiliruntu tantira tīraṉeṉṉum oru tūtuvaṉ
   pōntu pāṇṭi nāṭṭai vaḻutiyiṉiṭam oppikkumāṟu ceppa, varumaṉ
   ataṟkiṇaṅkāṉāyp pōrvēṇṭit tāṉum taṉatu karuvūrp patiyiṉkaṇuḷḷa pōr
   vīrarai aḻaittukkoṇṭu maturai varumāṟu āḷaṉuppiṉāṉ. karuvūr ceṉṟa
   cēvakaṉ varumuṉ vaḻutiyuñ cōḻaṉum paṭaiyoṭu vantu vaikaiyāṟṟiṉ
   vaṭakaraikkaṇ taṅkiṉar. cuntaraṉum oru viyūkattalaivaṉāka vantiruntāṉ.
   ataṉ piṟaku karuvūrc cēvakaṉ vantu 'avviṭattiṟ cēraṉ mantiri
   irācōpāyarukkum vaḻuti māmaṉār vīramārttāṇṭan toṇṭairācarukkum yuttam
   naṭantu koṇṭirutta viṟpaṭaivīrar varutaṟkillātu pōyiṟṟu' eṉṟāṉ. itu
   cevicāttiya varumaṉ taṉ mantiriyākiya cucīlaṉai viḷittu ālōcittu
   muṭiviṟ camātāṉañ ceyyumāṟu tāṉuñ cūcilaṉum mey kāppāḷaroṭu cōḻaṉ
   pācaṟaikkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ.
   iṅṅaṉam avarkaḷ ceṉṟu cōḻaṉ, vaḻuti, mutalāyiṉārō ṭellām
   uṭaṉpaṭikkaiyuṉ nipantaṉaikaḷaik kuṟittup pēci muṭittapiṉ, vaḻutiyum
   varumaṉum taṅkaḷukku nēriṭṭa makkaḷiḻaveṉṉum taurpākkiyattiṉaip paṟṟi
   iraṅkik koṇṭirukkum poḻutu, itukāṟum māṟuvēṭam pūṇṭu cuntaraṉ curūpaṉ
   eṉṟu māṟupeyaruṅ koṇṭirunta cuntarānantaṉum rūpāvatiyum tattamatu
   māṟuvēṭam nīttu uṇmai yuruvattoṭu veḷippaṭṭu muṟaiyē vaḻutiyaiyum
   varumaṉaiyum vaṇaṅki niṉṟārkaḷ! uṭaṉē āṇṭuk kuḻīi yiruntōrkaḷellām
   ikkāṭciyiṉaik kaṇṭu pērāṉantamuṟṟu em ālavāyp perumāṉatu aruḷai viyantu
   tutittaṉar! appoḻutu yāvarum naṇparāyiṉār. varumaṉum taṉatu kāṇāmaṟpōṉa
   makaḷai mīṭṭum peṟṟamaiyāṟ kaḻipēruvakaipūttu avviruvaratu maṇaviṉai
   muṭittaṟkuriya muyaṟcikaḷ ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. itaṉuṭaṉ nāṭakam
   muṭikiṉṟatu.
   mutaṟpatipiṟ pōlātu ippatippiṟ pāttiraṅkaḷiṉ ayal pukaṭkēṟṟa
   colvaḻakkum naṭaivēṟupāṭum ciṟcila tiruttaṅkaḷum putiya ceyyuṭkaḷum
   amaikkappaṭṭaṭuḷavāmāṟu yāvaruṅ kaṇṭupōṟṟutaṟkuriyatu. ciṟcila
   ceyyuṭkaḷiṟ pāṭapētaṅkaḷuñ ceytirukkiṉṟaṉam. palavakaiyiṉum innāṭakam
   ematu nāṭakaviyalōṭu ottiyaṅkumāṟu kāṇka.
   inta nāṭakamāṉatu maṉmata varuṭam āṭi mātam muppattiraṇṭāntēti
   viyāḻakkiḻamaiyaṉṟiravu, ceṉṉai vittiyāpimāṉa caṅkattār mūlamāy
   araṅkēṟṟappaṭṭatu. itaṉ kaṭkuṟṟaṅ kuṟaikaḷ irukkumāyiṟ pēraṟivuṭaiyōr
   piriyattōṭum avaiyiṟṟiṉait tirutti emmītu kirupai purivārāka.
   vi. kō. cū.
   ---------------------------
                     ___________________________________

                                  pāyiram

   -------
   vināyakar vaṇakkam
   kaṭṭaḷaikkalittuṟai
   poṟpuṟu rūpā vatikātai taṉṉaip pukaḻmikunta
   naṟpaya ṉāṭaka māppain tamiḻi ṉavilutaṟkuc
   ciṟpara vaḷḷaleñ cintā maṇiyaṉa cittitaruṅ
   kaṟpaka yāṉai caraṇāra vintaṅ karutuvamē. (1)
   cuppiramaṇiyar vaṇakkam
   vañciviruttam
   ulavuce luyartiṇaik kuriyaṉaik
   kalavaṭi yavararuṭ kaṭaliṉaik
   kulavuṟ pulavayiṟ kumaraṉaik
   kalaiyuṇar pulavaṉaik karutuvām. (2)
   nāmakaḷ vaṇakkam
   kaliviruttam
   kaṉṉirū pāvati kātai coṟṟiṭac
   coṉṉayam poruṇayan tōṉṟu nūlpala
   paṉṉuṟu pulavarnāp payilum pāmayil
   poṉṉaṭit tāmarai pōṟṟi vāḻkuvām. (3)
   naṭarācar vaṇakkam
   kalinilaittuṟai
   ulaka meṉṉumor nāṭaka cālaiyi ṉuyirā
   malaki lānaṭar taṅkaḷai* yatutina māṭṭi
   yilaku cūttira tāraṉā miṟaivaṉai yematu
   talaiva ṉānaṭa rācaṉait tāṇmalar paṇivām. (4)
   --------------------------
                     ___________________________________

   nāṭaka pāttiraṅkaḷ
   1. caṟkuṇaṉ : pāṇṭiyanāṭṭup pūrvarācaṉ.
       2. cūracēnaṉ : taṟkālattup pāṇṭināṭāḷuñ cēraṉ.
       3. vīrēntiraṉ : cēraṇāṭṭaracaṉ.
       4. cucīlaṉ : cūracēṉaṉ mantiri.
       5. nayavacanaṉ : oru kaṉavāṉ; cūracēnaṉatu naṇpaṉ.
       6. cuntarānantaṉ: caṟkuṇaṉ makaṉ; rūpāvati kātalaṉ.
       7. cukumāraṉ : cuntarānantaṉatu naṇpaṉ.
       8. cantiraḻakaṉ : nayavacanaṉ makaṉ; cuntarānantaṉatu nēcaṉ.
       9. vittiyācākarar : pāṇṭiyanāṭṭil oru tamiḻppulavar.
       10. ñānatīpar : pāṇṭiyanāṭaṭutta vaṉattil oru muṉivar.
       11. āṉmanātar : ñāṉatīparatu cīṭar.
       12. tantiratīraṉ : cōḻaṉ tūtuvaṉ.
       13. murukaṉ : maturaiyil vaiyāḷi vīti kāppōṉ.
       14. cuntari : caṟkuṇavaḻutiyiṉ maṉaivi.
       15. kōmaḷavalli : cūracēnavarmaṉ maṉaivi.
       16. mūpāvati : cūracēnaṉ makaḷ; nāṭakat talaivi.
       17. ampujāṭci : cucīlaṉ makaḷ; rūpāvatiyiṉ tōḻi.
       18. kanakamālai : rūpāvatiyiṉ tōḻi;
       cevili, cēṭiyar mutalāyiṉār.
       kaṉavāṉkaḷ, vēṭaṉ, cēvakar, cēvakar talaivaṉ, kāvalāḷar,
       meykāppaḷar mutalāyiṉār.

   nāṭaka nikaḻviṭam
   ----------------
   perumpāṉmai pāṇṭi nāṭṭut talai nakarākiya maturaiyilum ataṟkaṭutta
   kāṭṭilum, ciṟupāṉmai cōṇāṭṭut talainakarākiya tañcaiyilumām.
   -----------------------

               rūpāvati allatu kāṇāmaṟ pōṉa makaḷ

                                aṅkam - 1.
                              mutaṟ kaḷam.

   ------
   iṭam:- oru pūñcōlai
   kālam:- mālai
   pāttiram :- cuntarānantaṉ.
   cuntarānantaṉ: (taṉakkuḷ) pūkkaḷ malarntu vācaṉai vīcāniṉṟa
   iccōlaiyiṉkaṇ yāṉ vantu ulāvi mantamāyaṭikkiṉṟa iḷan teṉṟaṟ kāṟṟiṉ
   cukattai yanupavippataṟku eṉ maṉam oruppaṭukiṉṟa tillai. -eṉ ceyvēṉ!
   oru vārattiṟku muṉavaraiyiluṅ kaḷippuṟṟuc cerukkittirinta eṉatu ēḻai
   neñcamē! yāṉ cantōṣappaṭuvatai ēṉ taṭukkiṉṟāy? eṉmītu ciṟitu karuṇai
   kūrntu eṉ vicaṉamellām nīṅkumpaṭi ceyvatu niṉak kaḻakēyaṉṟō?
   (cukumāraṉuñ cantiramukaṉum varukiṉṟaṉar.)
   antō! pāvi maṉamē! nī tuyarappaṭuvatumaṉṟi eṉṉaiyum ēṉ varuttukiṉṟāy?
   (avarkaḷ piṉṉē niṟkiṉṟaṉar.)
   āṇṭu mayilka ḷāṭuvataiyum, kiḷikaḷ pēcik kulāvuvataiyum, nī pārttum
   uṉakku iṉpamillaiyō? -uṉṉaiyē uyirt tuṇaiyāyp peṟṟa eṉṉai nī ivvāṟu
   varuttukiṟpatu tarmamākumō?
   (pāṭukiṉṟāṉ)
   eṉṉuṭaṉ piṟantu meṉṉai yiṭarppaṭup patu*ṉ ṟēyō
       veṉṉacey vāṉkol pārppa leṉṟunī varuttu vāyō
       viṉṉalcey maṉamē yiṉṉu mirakkamvan tilatō veṉṟa
       ṉaṉṉaya naṇpa rēṉu kaṇṇiyiṅ kāṟṟi ṭārō? (5)

   (mauṉam)
   cukumāraṉ:- ē, cuntarānantā! iḵteṉṉai? mika viyappāyirukkiṉṟatē niṉ
   ceyal!
   cuntarānantaṉ:- eṉa tarumaiyāṉa cukumāraṉē! yāṉ eṉ ceyvēṉ? eṉmītu
   tavaṟoṉṟumillai. eṉ maṉattuṉpam nīṅkiṟṟillai.
   cantiramukaṉ:- nī yēḻunāḷāka ivvāṟutāṉ collukiṉṟāy!
   cukumāraṉ:- āvatu āyiṟṟu. nām iṉi yeṉ ceyvatu? varuttamiṉṟik kālaṅ
   kaḻippataṉṟō nalam?
   cuntarānantaṉ:- eṉatu piriya nēcarkāḷ! eṉ maṉavaruttam nāḷukku nāḷ
   atikappaṭukiṉṟatē yoḻiyak kuṟaikiṉṟatillai. eṉṉuṭaiya ūkkamellām eṅkēyō
   ōṭiviṭṭatu. eṉṉāṟ poṟukkak kūṭavillai.
   cantiramukaṉ:- nī kalvikaṟṟataṉ payaṉṟāṉeṉṉai? ettakaiya tuṉpaṅkaḷ
   nēriṭṭālum, avaṟṟaiyellām poṟumaiyuṭaṉ cakittuk koṇṭiruppataṉṟō
   mēṉmaiyāṉatu. ellān terinta uṉakku yāṉ collavum vēṇṭumō?
   cukumāraṉ:- ellām valla kaṭavuḷ iṉpa vaṭivāṉavar; avar cakala
   kāriyaṅkaḷaiyum maṉitaratu naṉmaikkākavē ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. ākaiyāl
   avaratu aruḷiṉāl nikaḻnta icceyalkaḷellām namakku naṉmaiyiṉaiyē
   payakkum eṉpatai nī aṟiyāyō?
   cuntarānantaṉ:- aṟivēṉ, aṟivēṉ.
   cantiramukaṉ:- aṟintiruntum nī varuntuvatu nēritō? nīyuṉ peṟṟōrai
   viṭṭup pirintiruppataṉāṟ pātakam eṉṉa? nīyum avarkaḷum vevvēṟiṭaṅkaḷi
   liruntapōtilum, orē yūrilē tāmē yirukkiṉṟīrkaḷ. aracaṉum nām
   niṉaikkiṟapaṭi avvaḷavu koṭiyavaṉalla ṉeṉṟuṅ kēḷvi. innāṭkaḷil oru
   nāṭṭaracaṉ iṉṉorunāṭ ṭaracaṉai veṉṟu avaṉaic ciṟaiyiliṭuvatu
   vaḻakkāṟaṉṟō?
   cuntarānantaṉ:- antō! antō! nīvirellām iṉṟu naṭanta camācārañ ciṟitun
   teriyātu ivvāṟu collukiṉṟīrkaḷē! aiyakō! eṉceyvēṉ! eṉceyvēṉ! teyvamē!
   cukumāraṉ:- veku āccariyamā yirukkiṉṟatē! eṅkaḷukkut teriyāta ceyti
   appaṭi nikaḻntateṉṉai?
   cuntarānantaṉ:- (pāṭukiṉṟāṉ)
   pukaḻntunīr pēcum puravalaṉ ṟaṉṉā
       ṉikaḻnta koṭuñcey ṇīṉaikkavoṇ ṇātē
       nīraṟi yīrō? nēcarkāḷ!
       eppaṭi yumakkiyāṉ ceppuval? antō! (6)
       tantaiyun tāyiṉaiyun taḷḷavoṇṇāp paruvattē
       cintaiyiṉiṟ ciṟitēṉu mirakkamilāt tīkkuṇattōṉ
       kantamalart tāḷkaḷilē kāṭṭiluḷa muḷḷēṟik
       kuntiyoru pālēṅkat turattiṉāṉ koṭumpāvi! (7)
       aṉṉaiyeṉuñ cuntariyā ḷariyavaṉat tuṟṟaṉaḷō
       paṉṉariya paṇimoḻiyāḷ paṭuttuyarva tentamarat
       tiṉṉiḻalō ventaipirin tēkiyatu melliṭamō
       veṉṉuṭaiya valviṉaiyō vittuyarppaṭ ṭāḻkiṉṟēṉ! (8)
       mantakō kilartiṉamum vantupayi lummiṉiya
       puttamutat tēmoḻiyāḷ pulikkiraiyāy viṭṭaṉaḷō
       vittaiyelāṅ kuṭikoṇṭa vippiṟantām piriyamutu
       mattareñcaṟ kuṇavaḻuti yarivāyppaṭ ṭaḻintaṉarō! (9)
       tantaiyē niṉṉuṭaiya tāmaraittā ṇōvāvō
       cuntariyē yeṉṟāyē cukanīṅkic curattiṉiṭat
       tintavarai varun tuvataṟ keṉṉatīṅ kiḻaittaṉaiyō
       ventavita meṉṉuyirai yiṉittāṅki yiruppēṉō! (10)

   [cuntarānantaṉ mūrccaipōy viḻukiṉṟāṉ.]
   iruvarum:- iḵteṉṉai! iḵteṉṉai! iḵteṉṉai putumai!!
   [iruvarum mūrccai teḷivikkiṉṟaṉar.]
   [pūñcōlaiyiṉ maṟṟoru cār, rūpāvati, kanakamālai,
   ampujāṭci yivarkaḷ varukiṉṟaṉar.]
   rūpāvati:- ampujāṭci! iṅkē camīpattilē yeṉṉavō aḻukaik kural kēṭṭatē!
   aḵteṉṉeṉṟu pōyp pārttuviṭṭu varuvamē?
   ampujāṭci:- ēṉ, kanakamālai! rūpāvatitāṉ pārkkalām vāveṉṟu
   kūppiṭukiṟāḷē! ellōrumāyp pōyttām pāppamē?
   kanakamālai:- āṉāṟ cari. nīṅkaḷ iraṇṭu pērum kūppiṭukiṟapōtu nāṉ māṭṭēṉ
   eṉpēṉō, nāṉ māṭṭēṉeṉṟāluntāṉ viṭuvīrkaḷō?
   rūpāvatiyum ampujāṭciyum:- cari, pōkalām vā.
   [ivarkaḷ pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
   cukumāraṉ:- appā! cuntarānantā! ippaṭi eṉmēṟ cāyntukoḷ. ē cantiramukā!
   ippuṟam vā, caṟṟuk kuḷircciyāyk kāṟṟu mēlē yaṭikkaṭṭum.
   cantiramukaṉ:- ēṉ, cuntarānantā! uṉakku uṭampu eṉṉa ceykiṟatu?
   [rūpāvati mutalāṉavar varukiṉṟaṉar.]
   cuntarānantaṉ:- ammā! appā! ammā!! appā!! koñcam poṟuṅkaḷ.
   rūpāvati:- iru, iru. iṅkētāṉ, iṅkētāṉ.
   ampujāṭci:- atō, anta mātavi marattiṉkīḻ mūṉṟupēr uṭkārntirukkiṟārkaḷ.
   oruvaṉ maṭiyiṟ pēraḻakuvāynta maṟṟoruvaṉ cāyntukoṇṭirukkiṟāṉ! pār.
   kanakamālai:- āmām. nām iraintu pēcak kūṭātu.
   rūpāvati:- meḷḷac cattappaṭāmal nāminta mallikaip pantarilēyē yoḷintuk
   koṇṭiruntu avarkaḷ eṉṉa ceykiṟārkaḷ eṉṟupārppōm - nil, nil. eṉṉavō,
   pēcukiṟārkaḷ. kēṭpōm.
   [ivarkaḷ oḷittup pārkkiṉṟaṉar.]
   cantiramukaṉ:- eṉṉai itu? oru nāḷu millāmal iṉṟaikku māttiram ippaṭi nī
   mūrccai yaṭaintu eṅkaḷai yellām varuttappaṭumpaṭi vaittuviṭṭāy! ēṉappā,
   cuntarānantā!
   cuntarānantaṉ:- (cukumāraṉ maṭiyai viṭṭu vilakikkoṇṭu) itaik kuṟittu
   ñāpaka mūṭṭātē; eṉṉai yoṉṟum ippoḻutu kēḷātē. nām iṉimēl, cukumārā!
   iṅkē tāmatañ ceyyak kūṭātu.
   cukumāraṉ:- antap payal ciṟaikkāppāḷaṉ koñcam nēraṅkaḻittup pōṉāl
   atikamāyt talaikīḻāy viḻukiṟāṉ. 'aracaṉiṭattil teriyap paṭuttuvēṉ'
   eṉkiṟāṉ.
   cantiramukaṉ:- āmām. cīkkiramāyp pōvōm. vāruṅkaḷ.
   [cuntarānantaṉ, cukumāraṉ, cantiramukaṉ ivarkaḷ pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
   rūpāvati:- ēṉ, kanakamālai! inta mūṉṟu pērum yār? nī yinta vūrilēyē
   irukkiṟavaḷ tāṉē! uṉṉālē, ivarkaḷ iṉṉār eṉṟu collak kūṭumē!
   ampujāṭci:- eṉṉaik kēḷēṉ. nāṉ collukiṟēṉ. avaḷ inta vūrukku vantu iṉṉum
   iraṇṭu varuṣaṅkūṭa ākavillai. avaḷuk kivarkaḷaippaṟṟi eṉṉa teriyap
   pōkiṉṟaṉa!
   kanakamālai:- ēṇṭiyammā! eṉakkum avarkaḷil oruttaṉait teriyum.
   rūpāvati:- ēṉ ivvaḷavu pēccu? nīṅkaḷ iraṇṭu pērum inta vūrilēyē
   irukkiṟavarkaḷ eṉṟu niṉaittuc coṉṉēṉ. ippōtu yār coṉṉāltāṉ eṉṉa?
   ampujāṭci! nītāṉ col.
   ampujāṭci:- illai, illai! avaḷukkut terinta maṭṭil avaḷē collaṭṭum;
   appuṟam vēṇṭumeṉṟāl avaḷukkut teriyātatu iruntāl nāṉ collukiṟēṉ.
   kanakamālai:- atoṉṟum illai. uṉakkuttāṉ naṉṟāyt teriyumē! collēṉ.
   rūpāvati:- cari, irukkaṭṭum. ivarkaḷ yār? cīkkiram col. kēṭpōm.
   ampujāṭci:- aḻakāy oru piḷḷai iṉṉoruttaṉ maṭiyilē uṭkārntu cāyntu
   koṇṭiruntāṉē, avaṉ tāṉ munti irunta rājā caṟkuṇavaḻutiyiṉ piḷḷai.
   kanakamālai:- avaṉaittāṉ eṉakkum teriyum.
   rūpāvati:- orutti collukiṟapōtu naṭuvilē pēcātē. uṉakkut terintāl
   appuṟam col.
   ampujāṭci:- avaṉ peyar cuntarānantaṉ. vilvittaiyil veku keṭṭik kāraṉām,
   paṭippil mikat tērntavaṉām. muṉṉirunta rājāvukku ivaṉ orē piḷḷai,
   ākaiyiṉālē mika arumaiyāyk kaṇṇukkuk kaṇṇāy ivaṉai vaḷarttu vantār.
   ivaṉai maṭiyilē uṭkārttikkoṇ ṭiruntāṉē avaṉ tāṉ eṉ takappaṉārōṭu kūṭap
   piṟanta attaip piḷḷai. avaṉ peyarai nāṉ collappaṭātu!
   kanakamālai:- avaṉukku vāḻkkaippaṭap pōkiṟāyō? atu tāṉ pēr colla māṭṭēṉ
   eṉkiṟāy pōlum!
   rūpāvati:- yārukku vāḻkkaippaṭap pōkiṟāḷ?
   kanakamālai:- avaḷuṭaiya attai piḷḷaikkām. iṉṉum kalyāṇam kūṭa
   ākavillai. ataṟkuḷḷē pēr collappaṭātu eṉkiṟāḷ!
   rūpāvati:- ēṇṭi ampujāṭci! avaiyellām irukkaṭṭum. nī aḻakāy oruvaṉ
   aracaṉ makaṉ eṉṟu coṉṉāyē avaṉ aḻuvāṉēṉ?
   ampujāṭci:- ellām uṉakkē terintirukkumē!
   rūpāvati:- illai, niccayamāyc collukiṟēṉ. eṉakkoṉṟum teriyātu.
   ampujāṭci:- āṉāṟ caritāṉ. oruvēḷai uṉakkut terintirukkumō veṉṟu
   niṉaittēṉ. uṅkaḷ takappaṉār intap pāṇṭiya rājyattai jeyittu intat
   tēcattu rājāvaiyum, rājā peṇṭāṭṭiyaiyum oru ciṟaiyilēyum, inta
   rājāpiḷḷai, ippōtu pārttōmē, avaṉai iṉṉoru ciṟaiyilēyum aṭaittu
   vaittiruntār. anta rājāvum rāṇiyum inta vūrilēyē irukkiṟataṉālē
   kalakaṅkaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa veṉṟu niṉaittu avarkaḷ iraṇṭu peyaraiyum iṉṟaik
   kālaitāṉ inta vūrukkaṭutta oru kāṭṭilē koṇṭupōyt turattiviṭṭu varumpaṭi
   uṅkaḷ tantaiyār uttaravu koṭuttārām. antappaṭiyē cēvakarkaḷum avarkaḷai
   aḻaittukkoṇṭu turatti viṭṭuvarak kāṭṭukkuppō yirukkiṟārkaḷām. intac
   camācārattaik kēṭṭuviṭṭuttāṉ cuntarānantaṉ aḻutatu. tāyār takappaṉār
   eṉṟāl avaṉukkup pirāṇaṉ! eṉṉa ceyvaṉ?
   rūpāvati:- ēṇṭi! mūrccaipōy viḻuntāṉāmē! avarkaḷ pēcik koṇṭārkaḷē!
   aḵteṉṉai! aḻutāl mūrccai pōkumō?
   kanakamālai:- tāyār takappaṉāraik kāṭṭut tuṣṭamirukaṅkaḷukku
   iraiyākumpaṭi vayatāṉa kālattilē turattiviṭṭa caṅkatiyaik kātilē kēṭṭum
   mūrccai pōkātavar yār? appērppaṭṭavar mikavum kaṉṉeñcarāyt tām
   irukkavēṇṭum.
   ampujāṭci:- anta cukumāraṉum, cantiramukaṉum, pakkattilē illā tiruntāl,
   cuntarānantaṉ, appāvi! varuttameṉpatai iṉṉateṉṟu aṟiyātavaṉ! veku
   avastaippaṭṭup pōyiruppāṉ. cuvāmi, avarkaḷaic camīpatti lirukkumpaṭi
   ceytārē! atuvaṉṟō vicēṣam!
   kanakamālai:- atellām irukkaṭṭum. appōtu māttiram akamuṭaiyāṉ peyaraic
   collappaṭā teṉṟāyē! ippōtu māttiram collalāmō?
   rūpāvati:- iṉṉum kalyāṇam ākavillaiyē! coṉṉāl eṉṉa? kuṟṟamillai.
   kanakamālai:- cantiramukaṉeṉṟu coṉṉāyē, avaṉ yār?
   ampujāṭci:- avaṉ tāṉ nayavacakar eṉpavaruṭaiya puttiraṉ. cuntarā
   nantaṉōṭukūṭap paṭittavaṉ. ivarkaḷum, eṅkaḷ attai piḷḷaiyum āka
   mūṉṟupeyarum vittiyācākarap pulavariṭam kalvi kaṟṟuk koṇṭārkaḷām.
   rūpāvati:- aḵtirukkaṭṭum. cuntarānantaṉaip paṟṟi ivvūrār eṉṉa collik
   koḷḷukiṟārkaḷ?
   kanakamālai:- cuntarānantaṉā? makāputticāli! ūrār collukiṟateṉṉa?
   naṉṟāyt terinta viṣayantāṉē!
   rūpāvati:- ampujāṭci! kanakamālai māttiram eṉṉavō ippaṭic collukiṟāḷ.
   nāṉum iraṇṭu mūṉṟu pēriṭattilē kēṭṭēṉ; avaṉ eṉṉavō veku tuṣṭaṉām. tāṉ
   tāṉ aracaṉ makaṉ eṉṟu eṇṇikkoṇṭu ūrmakkaḷai ellām piṭittukkoṇṭu aṭittu
   varuttappaṭuttuvāṉām. ippaṭi ellām collik koḷḷukiṟārkaḷē!
   ampujāṭci:- cuntarānantaṉaiyuñ colli maṟṟaip peyaraiyum collukiṟatā?
   veku naṉṟāyirukkiṟatu! avaṉā tuṣṭaṉām? appaṭic collukiṟavarkaḷ nākkup
   puḻuttuttāṉ pōkum. parama cīva kāruṇṇiya ṉeṉṟāl avaṉukkuttāṉ takum.
   kanakamālai:- appaṭi irukka, aḻaku aḻaku, yār coṉṉatō teriyavillai!
   ampujāṭci:- intappaṭi coṉṉāltāṉ ippōtu vantirukkira rājā cūracēnavarmar
   taṅkaḷ pērilē piriyamāyiruppāreṉṟu niṉaittārkaḷō! avarkaḷum naṉṟāyc
   coṉṉārkaḷ, rājāvum naṉṟāyk kēṭṭukkoṇṭār! vīṭṭilē irukkiṟapōtē vitivantu
   cūḻntatupōla, teyvamē eṉṟu irukkiṟavaṉ talaiyilē naṉṟāyp paḻiyaip
   pōṭṭārkaḷ! cuvāmi irukkiṟār ellāvaṟṟiṟkum! nām colliyā ākappōkiṟatu?
   kanakamālai:- ulaivāyai mūṭalām, ūrvāyai mūṭalāmō? collukiṟavarkaḷ
   colliviṭṭup pōkaṭṭum!
   ampujāṭci:- yār eṉṉa coṉṉapōtilum avaṉaip paṟṟi nammuṭaiya eṇṇam eṉṉavō
   māṟap pōkiṟatillai.
   rūpāvati:- eṉṉavō? ammā! ūrār coṉṉataic coṉṉēṉ. avaṉ nallavaṉāka
   iruntāl ellām nallatu tāṉē!
   kanakamālai:- eṉakkut terintamaṭṭil aḻaku, putti, cakti, kuṇam
   ivaṟṟilellām avaṉukkuc camāṉamāṉavarkaḷ illai eṉṟē collalām.
   rūpāvati:- cari, aḵtu appaṭi irukkaṭṭum, ampujāṭci ētāvatu oru pāṭṭup
   pāṭu kēṭpōm. naṉṟāy mantamārutam vīcukiṟatu. eṅkē! cukamāyp pāṭu,
   kēṭpōm.
   ampujāṭci:- (pāṭukiṉṟāḷ)
   (ka) irākam - intustāṉ kāpi. tāḷam - āti.
       pallavi
       nāyakaṉē varuvāy - ivvēḷaiyil
       caraṇaṅkaḷ
       cintai tiṉantoṟum       nontu varuntiṉēṉ
       cuntara mativataṉā       ivvēḷaiyil (nāyaka)
       maṉmata pāṇaṅkaḷ       mārpiṉiṟ pāyntaṉa
       naṉmaṉañ ceytaruvāy       ivvēḷaiyil (nāyaka)
       ittaṉai kūṟiyu       miḷakila tōneñca
       mottanal lāmaṉattāṉ       ivvēḷaiyil (nāyaka)
       caṉmārkkar vittiyā       cākarar tampatañ
       coṉmēlun tāmatamā       ivvēḷaiyil (nāyaka)

   rūpāvati:- camayattuk kēṟṟa pāṭṭum pāṭṭukkēṟṟa kuralum irākamum tāḷamum
   ellām naṉṟāyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
   kanakamālai:- avaḷukkup pāṭukiṟataṟkuk kēṭpāṉēṉ? jaṉmāntarattilē
   puṇṇiyam paṇṇi irukkiṉṟāḷ! atu tāṉ teyvam nalla kuralāyk
   koṭuttirukkiṟatu!
   ampujāṭci:- iṉṟaikku eṉṟu eṉṉavō koñcam toṇṭai kaṭṭik koṇṭirukkiṟatu,
   allāmaṟpōṉāl iṉṉum caṟṟu naṉṟāyp pāṭi irukkalām.
   rūpāvati:- pāṭṭuppāṭukiṟavarkaḷellārum aḵtu eṉṉaiyō teriyavillai,
   toṇṭai kaṭṭikkoṇṭiruntālum cari illāmaṟ pōṉālum cari, ippaṭittāṉ
   collukiṟārkaḷ.
   kanakamālai:- atu avarkaḷukku vaḻakkamāyp pōyviṭṭatu. ataṟku avarkaḷ
   eṉṉa ceyvārkaḷ?
   ampujāṭci:- cari. nām ippaṭip pēcikkoṇṭiruntāl, namakku nāḻikaiyāka
   villai? astamittup pōyiṟṟu. akattiṟkup pōka vēṇṭāmā?
   kanakamālai:- āmām, cūriyaṉ appōtē malaivāyilē viḻuntu viṭṭāṉē. cī, cī,
   iṉimēl irukkappaṭātu. ammā, eṅkaḷappā kōpittuk koḷvār.
   rūpāvati:- ām, eṉakkum nēramāyviṭṭatu.
   [yāvarum pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
   -------------------
                     ___________________________________

                     aṅkam - 1 / iraṇṭāṅkaḷam

   iṭam:- kāṭu
   kālam:- naṇpakal
   pāttiraṅkaḷ:- caṟkuṇaṉ, cuntari
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- iṉṉum koñcantūram pōṉāl oru vēlamara mirukkiṉṟatu, ataṉ
   niḻalil taṅkuvōm. ciṟitu viraivāy vā, aiyō! uṉ pātaṅkaḷ taraiyai
   mitittaṟiyāvē! uṉakku veyyileṉpatē teriyātē! eṉ ceyvēṉ! ituvum eṉ
   talaivitiyō? ā! teyvamē!
   cuntari:- teyvantāṉ eṉṉaceyyum? nāmtām eṉṉa ceyvōm? koñcat tūrattiṟ
   kappāl vēlamaram oṉṟu irukkiṟateṉṟīrkaḷē! aḵteppaṭi uṅkaḷukkut teriyum?
   caṟkuṇaṉ:_ antō! uṉṉaik kaippiṭitta intappāvi aracaṉāyirunta kālattiṟ
   palataram itē kāṭṭil vēṭṭaiyāṭa vantirukkiṉṟāṉ!
   cuntar:- veyyilō akōramāyk koḷuttukiṉṟatu! ippaṭiyē aḻutu koṇṭu
   pōvōmāṉāl uṭampu kaḷaittup pōyviṭum. appuṟam naṭakkamuṭiyātu.
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- aiyō! eṉkaṇṇē! uṉakku irukkiṟa aṟivu eṉakkillaiyē! eṉ
   ceyvēṉ!
   cuntari:- nammaip piramā avastaippaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟē niṉaittu mutalil
   irājya paripālaṉattai yellām koṭuttu ippōtu avaṟṟai yellām piṭuṅkik
   koṇṭu tuṣṭa mirukaṅkaḷukku iraiyākumpaṭi paṭaittuviṭṭāṉ! kāṭō makā
   kaṭōramā yirukkiṟatu! veyyilō iṉṟaikku eṉṟu makōkkiramā yirukkiṟatu!
   tiṉamum irunta tairiyamum iṉṟu cōtaṉai ceytatupōlak kai viṭṭup
   pōyviṭṭatu! pūrva jaṉmattil nām eṉṉa pāvam ceytōmō avaṟṟiṉ payaṉai
   ellām ippōtu aṉupavikkumpaṭi nēriṭṭu viṭṭatu! vaḷappamuḷḷa nāṭṭai
   iḻantōm! veku nērttiyāṉa maturai nakarai iḻantōm! alaṅkāramāṉa
   araṇmaṉaiyai iḻantōm! uṭaiyai iḻantōm! poruḷai iḻantōm! mīṉāṭci
   cuntarēcuvarar taricaṉattai iḻantōm! 'cuntarēcuvarar' eṉṟa uṭaṉē
   ñāpakam varukiṟatu. ellāp pākkiyattilum mēlāṉa nammuṭaiya arumaic
   cuntarā nantaṉaiyum iḻantōm! iḻantōm!! teyvamē! teyvamē!! cuntarānantā!
   cuntarānantā!! uṉṉai iḻantapiṟakum uyir tarippēṉō?
   [cuntari mūrccaipōy vīḻkiṉṟaṉaḷ.]
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- aiyō! eṉṉa ceyvēṉ! nī ciṟitu tairiyamā yirukkiṟāyē eṉṟu
   niṉaittēṉ. nīyu mivvāṟu mūrccai pōyviṭṭāyē! ivvaṉāntarattiṟ koñcam
   jalam, mayakkam tirumpumpaṭi uṉ mukattil teḷikkalām eṉṟālum
   jalamillaiyē! eṅkēyāvatu cuṟṟip pārttukkoṇṭu varuvōm eṉṟālum uṉṉait
   taṉiyāy viṭṭuviṭṭup pōṉāṟ puliyaṭittukkoṇṭu pōy viṭṭā leṉceyvēṉ!
   kaṭaināḷil, nāṭu, nakar ellāvaṟṟaiyum tuṟantēṉ! arumputalvaṉai yiḻantu,
   puttiracōka muṟṟēṉ! kaṭaiciyil eṉ uyirt tuṇaiviyākiya cuntariyaiyum
   iḻakka vēṇṭumō! vēṇṭumō!! cuntarī! cuntarī!! eṉṉai ippaṭi ēkāṅkiyāy
   viṭṭup pirital niyāyamō?
   kalaiyelāṅ kaṟṟamātē pōtiyō kaṭiya neñcēṉ
       talaiviyai yiḻanta piṉṉuñ cākilē ṉirukkiṉṟēṉē! (11)

   (mauṉam) 'aiyā' eṉṟa captam kēṭṭēṉ. ā! yārō pārppēṉ.
   [oru vēṭaṉ varukiṉṟāṉ.]
   vēṭaṉ:- ēṉ aiyā! ippaṭi yaḷukiṟiya? uṅkaḷukku - ākākākā! yārō ammā!
   eṉṉa mayakkamō? itō taṇṇiyirukkutu. teḷiyuṅka mukattilē.
   [vaḻutiyār taṇṇīr teḷikkiṉṟār.]
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- koñcam ammāvukku veyyil maṟaivāy nil appā!
   [vēṭaṉ avvāṟu niṟṟalum, vaḻutiyār tūkkimēlē cāyttuk koḷkiṉṟār; cuntari
   mūrccai teḷikiṉṟāḷ.]
   cuntari:- hā! hu!
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- eṉ pirāṇa nāyakiyē! eṉakku tairiyañ coṉṉāyē! nī ivvāṟu
   mūrccaipōy eṉṉait tuṉpappaṭuttutal cariyākumō? nī yappaṭiyē uyir
   tuṟantiruppaiyāyiṉ yāṉtāṉ piḻaippēṉō? ivaiyellām emperumāṉ viḷaiyāṭē
   yallavō? "avaṉiṉṟi yōraṇuvum acaiyātu" eṉṟa periyōr vacaṉam poyttup
   pōkumō? eṉmuttē! eṉmītu cāyntu koṇṭu caṟṟu iḷaippāṟuvāy!
   vēṭaṉ:- aiyā! iṅkē pulikaraṭi mutaliya pirāṇikaḷ cuttittiriyumē iṅkē
   yirukkak kūṭātē-
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- nāṅkaḷ cākavēṇṭiyatum kaṭavuḷukku iṣṭamāṉāl atuvum naṭantē
   tīrum. ataṟkāka nām payappaṭuvatēṉ?
   cuntari:- appaṭiyillai. nām nammālāṉa muyaṟcikaḷaic ceytāl
   naṭakkiṟapaṭi naṭakkaṭṭum. kaṭavuḷ nammuṭaiya ceyalkaḷ mūlamākavētāṉ
   namakku naṉmai yiṉaiyun tīmaiyiṉaiyum viḷaikkiṉṟār. ākaiyāl nām oṉṟuñ
   ceyyātiruntāl namakku naṉmai viḷaivateppaṭi?
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- eṉatarumait talaiviyē! nī colvaṉa vellām niyāyantām. nām
   ippoḻutu taṅki yiruppataṟku iṭam eṅkē?
   vēṭaṉ:- aiyā! nīṅka atukku yōcaṉai ceyyavēṇṭām. itukkarukālē iraṇṭu
   nāḷiyavaḷit tūrattilē oru cāmiyār cāvaṭiyirukkutu. ippō uṅkaḷaik kūṭṭip
   pōy antac cāmiyār maṭattilē viṭṭuppōṭṭu appuṟam vīṭṭukkup pōṟēṉ.
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- antac cāmiyār peyareṉṉa?
   vēṭaṉ:- atu eṉakkut teriyātu cāmi. avar cāvaṭi māttiran teriyum.
   varukiṟatiṇṭā vāṅka.
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- koñcam poṟuttukkoḷ, appā! varukiṟōm:- ēṉ, puṟappaṭuvamā?
   cuntari:- iṉṉum evvaḷavu tūram irukkum appā! antac cāvaṭi?
   vēṭaṉ:- itō! kiṭṭattilē tāṉ ammā!
   cuntari:- āṉāṟ cari.
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- mella! mella! eṉ kaiyaip piṭittukkoḷ.
   vēṭaṉ:- nāṉ muṉṉukkup pōṟēṉ. piṉṉukku nīṅka metuvā vāṅka.
   [yāvarum pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
   -------------
                     ___________________________________

                     aṅkam - 1 / mūṉṟāṅkaḷam

   iṭam:= kāṭṭil muṉivarācciramam
   kālam:- mālai
   pāttiraṅkaḷ:- ñāṉatīpar, āṉmanātar
   ñāṉatīpar:- appā! ātmanātā! ceyyavēṇṭiya kāriyattaiyellāñ ceytu
   viṭṭāyā? civañānapōta cūttiraṅkaḷai nēṟṟuc ciravaṇam paṇṇiṉāyē!
   iṉṟaikku avaṟṟai ellām maṉaṉam paṇṇiviṭṭāyā? mēṟ pōkalāmā?
   āṉmanātar:- ākñāppirakāram, cakala kāriyaṅkaḷaiyuñ campūrṇappaṭutti
   viṭṭēṉ. ciravaṇam paṇṇiṉa cūttiraṅkaḷiṟ cila maṉattil
   avanāhappaṭavillai. ākaiyāl avai muḻuvataiyum maṉaṉam paṇṇat
   taṭaippaṭṭatu. ākaiyā leṉṉai kṣamittu eṉmītu mīṭṭuṅ kirupai kūravēṇṭum.
   ñānatīpar:- antap pirakāramē, uṉakku maṟupaṭiyum maṉattiṟ paṭumpaṭi
   collukiṉṟēṉ. itaṟku muṉṉē śrīharananta civācāriyar paḻukkap paḻukka
   neruppilē kāynta iruppu mukkāliyilē vīṟṟiruntu tiruvāy malarntaruḷiya
   civaparatva nirūpaṇakalōka pañcakam paṭaṉamāyiṟṟā?
   āṉmanātar:- tiruvuḷap pirakāram, paṭaṉamāyiṟṟu. kaṭṭaḷaiyaḷittāṟ
   collukiṉṟēṉ.
   [caṟkuṇaṉ, cuntari, vēṭaṉ ivarkaḷ viḷikkiṉṟaṉar.]
   ñānatīpar:- atirukkaṭṭum. yārō vācalilvantu 'cāmī! cāmī!' eṉṟu
   kūppiṭukiṟatu pōlat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ateṉṉaveṉṟu pōyppārttu vā.
   [āṉmanātar veḷiyē pōy varukiṉṟār.]
   āṉmanātar:- yārō iruvar, stirīpumāṉkaḷ, veyyilāṟ kaḷaittavar,cevviya
   mēṉiyar, ciṟanta nōkkiṉar, vāṭiya mukattiṉar, aḻuta kaṇṇiṉar, vantuḷar.
   avarkaḷ camīpattil oru vēṭaṉum niṟkiṉṟāṉ.
   ñāṉatīpar:- avviruvarum oruvēḷai namatu āciramattil taṅkalām eṉṟu
   vantārkaḷ pōlum! nīpōy avarkaḷai yiṅkē yiṭṭukkoṇṭu vantu viṭṭuviṭṭu
   avarkaḷukku ētēṉum pōjaṉattiṟku ēṟpāṭu cey.
   āṉmanātar:- appaṭiyē ceykiṉṟēṉ.
   [pōkiṉṟār.]
   (vēṭaṉoḻiya maṟṟa yāvarum varukiṉṟaṉar.)
   [caṟkuṇaṉuñ cuntariyum oruṅkē muṉivarai vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.]
   ñānatīpar:- tīrkkāyuṣmāṉ pava!- tīrkka cumaṅkalīpava!-nīṅkaḷiruvarum
   rāja lakṣaṇaṅkaḷōṭu kūṭiṉavarkaḷā yirukkiṉṟīrkaḷē! uṅkaḷukku intak kati
   campavippāṉēṉ?
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- cuvāmikāḷ! aṭiyēṉ pāṇṭiya tēcat taracaṉāvēṉ. ālāciyakṣēttira
   vāciyā yiruntēṉ. caṟkuṇap peyariṉēṉ. ivaḷ eḷiyēṉatu pāriyai yāvāḷ.
   cuntarip peyariṉāḷ. toṇṭai nāṭṭaracaṉ vīramārttāṇṭaṉuṭaiya makaḷ. cēra
   tēcātipati cūracēṉavarmaṉāṟ ceyikkappaṭṭu ivvāraṇiyattiṟkut tuṣṭa
   mirukaṅkaḷukku iraiyākumpaṭi turattappaṭṭōm. vaḻiyiloru vēṭaṉaic
   cantittōm. avaṉatu utaviyāl tēvarīratu āciramattiṟku vantu cērntōm.
   ñānatīpar:- icceyti umatu māmaṉārākiya toṇṭai nāṭṭaracaṉukkut teriyātō?
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- teriyātu. veṭkampaṟṟi avariṭam utavi kēṭkavillai. yāṉē
   pōrkkaḷam ceṉṟu yuttattiṟ ṟolaivuṇṭēṉ.
   ñānatīpar:- ippōtu ikkati ataṉiṉum veṭkak kēṭāyiṟṟē! ippaṭiyirukkalāmā?
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- ām. eṉ ceyvatu?
   ñānatīpar:- ippōtu umakku toṇṭai nāṭṭiṟkup pōy vatiya iṣṭamā? allatu
   cōḻa nāṭṭiṟkup pōy vatiya iṣṭamā? eṉakkut taṟkālattil rājarīkañceytu
   varukiṉṟa vīrēntira cōḻaṉait teriyum.
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- tēvarīratu iṣṭa meppaṭiyō appaṭiyē naṭakkak kāttirukkiṉṟēṉ.
   ñānatīpar:- āṉāl uṅkaḷiruvaraiyum cōḻa nāṭṭiṟku aṉuppuvatā
   yuttēcittirukkiṟēṉ. antap pirakāramē ceyvīrkaḷeṉṟu nampukiṉṟēṉ.
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- ā, appaṭiyē yākaṭṭum.
   ñānatīpar:- appā! ātmanātā!
   āṉmanātar:- itō! vantuviṭṭēṉ.
   [āṉmanātar varukiṉṟār.]
   ñānatīpar:- ivarkaḷiruvarum taṅkiyiruppataṟku vacatiyāṉa ōriṭam pārttu
   ivarkaḷukkuk koṭu. ivarkaḷukkup pōjaṉa mutalāṉavaikaḷum ceytu vai.
   āṉmanātar:- ākñāp pirakāram ceykiṟēṉ.
   caṟkuṇaṉ:- ē, pirāṇacuntari! cākṣāt mīṉāṭci cuntarēcuvarar kirupai
   nampēriṟ paripūraṇamā yirukkiṉṟa teṉpataṟkuc cantēkamēyillai. pār!
   nammuṭaiya jaṉmāntara pāvattiṉāl yām aṉupavikkumpaṭi nēriṭṭa
   tuṉpaṅkaḷai yellām nīkkuvataṟkup paramapatiyākiya civaperumāṉār, nī
   mūrccaipōy viḻuntapōtu oru vēṭaṉaik koṇarntu viṭṭār! piṟaku
   taṅkuvataṟku mikavum vacatiyāṉa oru muṉipuṅkavaruṭaiya āciramattaik
   koṇarntu viṭṭār! nām ittaṉai nāḷāka irājya paripālanam paṇṇiyum inta
   muṉicirēṣṭar ituvaraikkum inta vaṉattil iruppatu namakkut
   teriyātiruntatē! inta ñāṉatīparuṭaiya ācirvātamum namakkuk
   kiṭaikkumpaṭiyum antac cantiracēkararē ceytirukkiṉṟār. tiruvaruṭ
   kaṟpaka teyvacikāmaṇi (pāṭukiṉṟāṉ.
   eṉṉēniṉ karuṇai! eṉṉēniṉ karuṇai!
       taṉṉēril poruḷē! tātāyeṉ ṉiṟaiyē! (12)

   cuntari:- ataṟkum aiyamuṇṭō? kaṭampavanēcar karuṇaiyiṉār cakalamum
   naṉmaiyāy māṟak kaṭavaṉa. muṉivaruṭaiya karuṇai yeṉṉē!
   [yāvarum pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
   --------------
                     ___________________________________

                      aṅkam - 1 / nāṉkāṅkaḷam

   iṭam:- pāṇṭiyavaraṇmaṉai
   kālam:- kālai
   pāttiraṅkaḷ:- cūracēnaṉ, cucīlaṉ
   cūracēnaṉ:- nām intap pāṇṭiya nāṭṭaik kavarntu nām maturaiyampatiyai
   namatu rācatāṉi yākkikkoṇṭu nām navarattiṉaṅkaḷā liḻaikkappeṟṟa
   poṟpīṭattiṉmītu vīṟṟiruntu nām aracu purivatu ittēcattār yāvarkkum
   cantōṣantāṉē? ēṉ? cucīlarē?
   cucīlaṉ:- tāṅkaḷ caṟkuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉai veṉṟa makāvīrar! taṅkaḷ irāja
   rīkattukkuk kuṟai colluvāru muḷarō? innakaruḷār yāvarum tāṅkaḷ inta
   maturāpuriyaiyē talainakarākkik koṇṭō meṉṟu paṟaiyaṟaivitta aṟṟaināḷ
   aṭainta āṉantattiṟ kōraḷavu muṇṭō?
   cūracēnaṉ:- eṉṉa! iṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉaiyum avaṉ maṉaiviyaiyuṅ kāṭṭil
   turattiviṭṭuvarumpaṭi pōṉa cēvakarkaḷ varavillai!
   cucīlaṉ:- cēvakarkaḷ varukiṟa camayantāṉ. itō vantu viṭuvārkaḷ.
   cūracēnaṉ:- nām avarkaḷaik kāṭṭil turattiviṭṭu varumpaṭi uttaravu
   koṭutta camācāram ūrārukkut teriyumō?
   cucīlaṉ:- teriyumpōlattāṉ tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
   cūracēnaṉ:- avarkaḷukku eppaṭi terintirukkak kūṭum?
   cucīlaṉ:- eppaṭit terintatō? namakkeṉṉa? payamā? aḵtoṉṟu millaiyē.
   cūracēnaṉ:- nām payattiṟkākak kēṭkavillai. teriyakkūṭiya vaḻi
   yeppaṭiyōveṉṟu terintukoḷḷat tām kēṭṭōm.
   cucīlaṉ:- jaṉaṅkaḷ; ēḻaikkuṭikaḷ! avarkaḷ mēlum nallavarkaḷ.
   cūracēnaṉ:- cari, ataṟkeṉṉa ippōtu? nayavacanareṅkē ituvaraiyiluṅ
   kāṇōm.
   cucīlaṉ:- itō vantuviṭṭārē! pāruṅkaḷ.
   [nayavacanaṉ varukiṉṟār.]
   cūracēnaṉ:- ō! nayavacanarē! umakku vayatu nūṟu! nāṅkaḷ niṉaikkavum
   nīrum vantīrē!
   nayavacanaṉ:- makārājā avarkaḷ camūkattiṟku aṭiyēṉ vantaṉam.
   cūracēnaṉ:- nirampa cantōṣam. ippaṭiyuṭkārum.
   nayavacanaṉ:- ākñaippaṭiyē!
   cūracēnaṉ:- nām tōṟkaṭittōmē, antap pāṇṭiyaṉ avaṉ puttiraṉ. avaṉ
   pēreṉṉa? cuntarānantaṉ, ām cuntarānantaṉ. avaṉukkut tōḻarākavum
   veḷiyilē pōkumpōtu kāvalāḷarākavum, iraṇṭupērai niyamittōmē, avarkaḷil
   oruttaṉ umatu kumāraṉ - maṟṟoruvaṉ - cucīlarvakaiyō? caritāṉ, caritāṉ.
   eṉṉattiṟkākak kēṭṭēṉeṉṟāl teriyātavarkaḷāyiruntāl oru vēḷai avaṉukku
   uṭaṉpāṭāyiruntu ētēṉum viparītañ ceyyakkūṭum; ataṟkākattāṉ.
   nayavacanaṉ:- (taṉakkuḷ) evvaḷavu nayavañcakaṉ! kapaṭaṉ! cūtukkāraṉ!
   kolaiñaṉ!
   cucīlaṉ:- nām yāvaṟṟiluṅ kavaṉamāyiruppatu niyāyantāṉē?
   cūracēnaṉ:- ēṉ aiyā - cucīlarē! ummuṭaiya vakai maṉuṣaṉ peyareṉṉa?
   cucīlaṉ:- evaṉaic collukiṉṟīrkaḷ?
   cūracēnaṉ:- avaṉtāṉ ciṟaiccālaiyilē kiṭakkiṉṟāṉē! avaṉ tōḻaṉ.
   cucīlaṉ:- eṉṉuṭaiya tamakkai piḷḷaitāṉ; avaṉ peyar cukumāraṉ.
   cūracēnaṉ:- inta ūrilē nalla tamiḻ vittuvāṉkaḷ irukkiṉṟārkaḷā?
   cucīlaṉ:- ēṉ ataṟkeṉṉa? camastāṉa cūriyum makā vittuvāṉumākiya
   vittiyācākarap pulavar irukkiṉṟār.
   cūracēnaṉ:- nām pāṇṭiyaṉōṭu purinta yuttattil aṭainta veṟṟiyaip paṟṟik
   kavipāṭumpaṭi avarkku uttaravu koṭukkavēṇṭum. appaṭiyallāviṭṭāl
   nammuṭaiya karuvūrc camastāṉa vitvāṉ vaṇṇak kaḷañciyap pulavarukku
   uttaravu koṭuppōm.
   cucīlaṉ:- ākaṭṭum. antappaṭiyē vittiyācākarap pulavariṭan taṅkaḷuṭaiya
   uttaraviṉait terivikkiṉṟēṉ.
   [cēvakaṉ varukiṉṟāṉ.]
   cēvakaṉ:- makārācā avuka camūkattilē kumpiṭṟēṉ.
   cūracēnaṉ:- aṭē! cēvakā! ēṉ ivvaḷavu tāmatam?
   cēvakaṉ:- makārācā! avukaḷaikkūṭṭi veku tolaipōy, vantavaḷi teriyāmē,
   naṭukkāṭṭilē, veyyil koḷuttak koḷuttak koṇṭu viṭṭiṭṭu vantēṉ. atutāṉ
   nēramāyiṟiccu.
   cūracēnaṉ:- nēṟṟē pōṉāyēṭā?
   cēvakaṉ:- āmām makārācā! nāṉ tirumpi vāraiyilē iruṭṭip pōyiṟiccu.
   atutāṉ ippa vanteṉ. illāṭṭi appavē vantiruppēṉ.
   cūracēnaṉ:- nī avarkaḷaik kāṭṭilē turattiviṭṭu varukiṟapōtu avarkaḷ
   eṉṉa pēcikkoṇṭārkaḷ? uḷḷataic collavēṇṭum. illāviṭil uṉ talaiyai inta
   vāḷiṉāl tuṇṭittup pōṭuvēṉ.
   [vāḷaiyōccukiṉṟāṉ.]
   cēvakaṉ:- makārācāvē! nāṉ avukaḷaik kāṭṭilē viṭṭavuṭaṉē avuka kōoṇṭu
   vāyviṭṭuk katṉāka. anta vēḷaiyilē puli karaṭi vantu tiriyumpōlat
   tōṇiccu. kāṭō veku payammāyirunticcu. ākaiyiṉālē, eṅkē puli eṉṉaiyum
   aṭiccukkiṭṭu pōyirumō eṇṭu payantu nāṉ eṉṉālē muṭiñcamaṭṭum veku vēkamā
   ōṭiyāntiṭṭēṉ.
   cūracēnaṉ:- avarkaḷ eṉṉaṭā pēcikkoṇṭārkaḷ eṉṟu kēṭṭāl ivaṟṟaiyellām
   uṉṉai yār collac coṉṉārkaḷ? ēṇṭā? avarkaḷ pēcikkoṇṭataic collukiṉṟāyā
   eṉṉa ippōtu?
   cēvakaṉ:- makārācāvē! nīṅkaḷ eṉṉaik koṇṭāluñ cari viṭṭāluñ cari. nāṉ
   uḷḷataittāṉ colṟēṉ. poy collallai. avuka vimmi vimmiyaḷutākaḷē yoḷiya
   vēṟē etuvum pēcallai.
   cūracēnaṉ:- nijantāṉā?
   cēvakaṉ:- āmām. makārācā! nicantāṉ.
   cucīlaṉ:- makārājā avarkaḷukkaṉṟō nāṉ colluvatu?
   cūracēnaṉ:- eṉṉa collukiṟīr? cucīlarē! collum, kēṭpōm.
   cucīlaṉ:- avarkaḷ naṭukkāṭṭiṟ pōṉa uṭaṉē avarkaḷukku aḻattōṉṟumē tavira
   vēṟoṉṟum pēcat tōṉṟātu. avarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ aṭainta tuṉpaṅkaḷai
   niṉaikkumpōtellām taṅkaḷaiaṟiyāmalē aḻuvārkaḷ. aḻutu, āṟi yōynta piṟaku
   tāṉ etāvatu pēcukiṟataṟkum, yōcaṉai ceyvataṟkum tōṉṟum; ataṟku muṉṉē
   vēṟoṉṟum muṭiyātu. inta viṣayaṅkaḷai yellām koñcam uḷanūṟ
   payiṟciyuṭaiyavarkaḷ eḷitiluṇarvārkaḷ. mēlum taṅkaḷukku immātiriyāṉa
   taurp pākkiyaṅkaḷai anupavikkumpaṭi nēriṭavum illai. ākaiyiṉāl tāṉ
   taṅkaḷukku itu putitākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
   cūracēnaṉ:- namakkēṉ intamātiriyāṉa kaṣṭaṅkaḷellām varum? nāmtām
   eppōtum evaṉ yuttattiṟku vantapōtilum caṉṉattamā yirukkiṟōmē!
   namakkeṉṉa?
   cucīlaṉ:- taṅkaḷukkētāvatu varumeṉṟu collavillai. tāṅkaḷ
   itaiyanupavittē yaṟivīrkaḷeṉṟu tāṉ coṉṉēṉ.
   cūracēnaṉ:-ām cucīlarē! nām aṉupavittatē illai.
   cucīlaṉ:- nāṉ inta viṣayattaic coṉṉatu etaṟkāka veṉṟāl nammuṭaiya
   cēvakaṉ collukiṉṟaṉa vellām vāstavamē\ eṉṟu kāṭṭum poruṭṭut tāṉ.
   mēlumivaṉ poy collukiṟavaṉ allaṉ.
   cūracēnaṉ:- aṭē! cēvakā! pōṭā caritāṉ.
   [cēvakaṉ pōkiṉṟāṉ.]
   cūracēnaṉ:- cucīlarē! nammuṭaiya eṇṇañcariyāy muṭintu viṭṭataṉṟō? iṉi
   nām pōkalāmā? eṉṉa? nayavacakarē! mauṉamāyirukkiṉṟīr!
   nayavacakaṉ:- cakalamum cari. (taṉakkuḷ) makārājā avarkaḷ ceyyuṅ
   kāriyaṅkaḷukkuk kēṭpāṉēṉ!
   [yāvarum pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
   -----------------------------------------------------------
                     ___________________________________

                       aṅkam - 1 / aintāṅkaḷam

   iṭam:--nayavacanaṉ vīṭu
   kālam:--piṟpakal
   pāttiraṅkaḷ:-- nayavacanarum, cila kaṉavāṉkaḷum
   mutaṟkaṉavāṉ:-- ēṉ! aiyā! nayavacanarē! nām itu varaiyilum intac
   cūracēṉaṉai nītimāṉā yiruppāṉeṉṟu eṇṇi iruntataṟku nērvirōtamāka
   nammuṭaiya pūrva rājā caṟkuṇa vaḻutiyaiyum, avar maṉaivi
   cuntariyāraiyum ciṟaiccālaiyil tāṉ aṭaittirukkiṟāṉ eṉṟu niṉaittōm.
   iṉṟaikku nāṉ vicāritta pōtu vēṟuvitamāka mantiri cucīlar vīṭṭu
   vēlaikkārarkaḷ pēcikkoṇṭārkaḷām; ataikkēṭṭu eṉṉuṭaiya vēlaikkāraṉ iṉṟu
   eṉṉiṭañ coṉṉāṉ. aḵtu eṉṉa nijamāyirukkumā?
   iraṇṭāṅkaṉavāṉ:-- eṉṉa aiyā! nīr eṉṉavō collukiṟīr? nām nāḷaikkē
   takunta ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceytu inta makāpāviyai ūraiviṭṭut turattiviṭṭu
   vaḻutiyāraik kūppiṭuvōm. nīrmāttiran tairiyamāyirum. oru kai pārppōm!
   nayavacanaṉ:-- ām. avaiyellā mirukkaṭṭum. nīr uṅkaḷuṭaiya eṇṇattai
   niṟaivēṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟāl intamātiri kaṇṭapaṭi pēcuvatiṟ pirayōcaṉamillai.
   cūracēnaṉō, pōr vīrarōṭu ippōtiṅkirukkiṉṟāṉ. antappōr vīrarkaḷai vēṟu
   eṅkēyāvatu aṉuppuvataṟkut takunta yuktikaḷ ceytu avarkaḷ pōṉa piṟaku
   nīṅkaḷ kalakamō kilakamō ētōceytu rājāvai--tīrttu viṭṭīrkaḷāṉāl appuṟam
   ellām cariyāyppōm. ( pāṭukiṉṟāṉ)
   'irāma ṉāḷiṉe ṉirāvaṇā ṉāḷiṉeṉ?
       nantam pāṭṭaic cintit tiruppōm'
       eṉṟu karutuvō roṉṟiṉu mutavār,
       araca ṉallaṉēṟ paraci yoḻukalām;
       aṉṟit tīyaṉēṟ poṉṟuta ṉalamām-- (13)
       cutta vīrar cuyāya meṇṇavar
       tatta nāṭṭiṉ taṉinala kāṭupu
       mottu pōriṉ muṉain tuyāṉ kaiyiṟkoṇ
       ṭetti ṟampuka ḻēyntuvīḻ kiṉṟaṉar! (14)

   mūṉṟāṅkaṉavāṉ:-- pōm, aiyā! pōm. kokkiṉ talaiyil veṇṇeyai vaittu aḵtu
   uruki appuṟam kokkup piṭikkiṟa tirukkaṭṭum! nīṅkaḷ māttiram
   pēcātiruṅkaḷ nāṉ tairiyamāy rājā vītiyiṟ pavaṉivarum pōtu ivvīṭṭiyiṉāṟ
   kuttip--pōṭukiṟēṉ. appuṟam mēl naṭakkiṟataip pārttukkoḷvōm. eṉṉai
   vēṇṭumeṉṟāṟ kolluvārakaḷ. nāṉ pōṉāṟ pōkiṉṟēṉ! nāṭukāppiṉ poruṭṭu oruvaṉ
   maṭṭiṟ cātal nalamaṉṟō?
   mutaṟkaṉavāṉ:-- ellāvaṟṟiṟkum nām avacarappaṭakkūṭātu. nayavacakar
   kūṟuvataiyum kēṭṭu naṉṟāy ālōcaṉai paṇṇi appuṟam nammuṭaiya eṇṇattai
   niṟaivēṟṟikkoḷḷalām. pataṟāta kāriyam citaṟātu!--āmām. pārttukkoḷḷum.
   iraṇṭāṅkaṉavāṉ:-- avaruṭaiya tātpariyam eṉṉaveṉṟāl, tāmacañ ceyvōmāṉāl
   oruvēḷai aracaṉukkut terintu avaṉ mikavum cākkiratai yāyiruntu
   viṭuvāṉākil nām niṉaitta eṇṇam niṟaivēṟātu pōyviṭumē eṉpatutāṉ.
   vēṟoṉṟumillai.
   mutaṟkaṉavāṉ:-- ivvaḷavum aracaṉukkut terintāl tāṉē kāriyam niṟaivēṟātu
   pōkum?
   iraṇṭāṅkaṉavāṉ:-- eṉṉavō? yār yārai nampalām? evaṉ eppaṭiyō?
   aracaṉuṭaiya pirīti campātikkiṟataṟkāka enta vañcakaṉāvatu inta
   yōcaṉaiyaippōy antap pātakaṉiṭam colli vaikkaṭṭum. ataiviṭa vēṟu viṉai
   vēṇṭām. namakkuttāṉkēṭeṉṟu niṉaikka vēṇṭām. nayavacanarukkuṅ keṭutal;
   ūr muḻuvatuñ cuṭṭup pocukkic cāmpalākkip pōṭuvāṉ.
   nayavacakaṉ:-- āmām. ataṟkāka ippōtu eṉṉa ceyyavēṇṭu meṉkiṟīr?
   mūṉṟāṅkaṉavāṉ:-- atutāṉ nāṉ appōtē piṭittuc collukiṉṟēṉē! avaṉaiccāykka
   nāṉāyiṟṟu eṉṟēṉē!
   nayavacanaṉ:-- ellāvaṟṟiṟkum nāṉoṉṟu collukiṉṟēṉ. kēḷuṅkaḷ. iṉṟaikku
   intac camācārattai, uṅkaḷuṭaiya yōcaṉaiyai, nammuṭaiya iḷavaracaṉ
   cuntaranantaṉukku eṉṉuṭaiya kumāraṉ cantiramukaṉ mūlamākac
   colliyaṉuppuvōm. cakalattiṟkum avaṉ eṉṉa collukiṟāṉō ataiyuṅ kēṭṭu
   naṭappōm. itaṟku eṉṉa collukiṟīrkaḷ? uṅkaḷukku ellām ippaṭic ceyyac
   cammatantāṉā?
   yāvarum:-- paramacammatam. appaṭiyē ceyvōm.
   nayavacanaṉ(eḻunatu) āṉāl eṉkumāraṉ cantiramukaṉiṭañ colli yaṉuppic
   cuntaranantaṉuṭaiya apippirāyattait terintu koṇṭu uṅkaḷukku yāṉ
   nāḷaikkuc collukiṟēṉ. itu viṣayam irakaciyamāyirukkaṭṭum veḷiyēṟiviṭap
   pōkiṟatu. pattiram! pattiram!!
   yāvarum:-- antappaṭiyē mikavuñ cākkirataiyā yirukkiṟōm. ataip paṟṟi
   nīṅkaḷ yōcikkavē vēṇṭām.
   [ yāvarum pōkiṉṟaṉar]
   mutalaṅkam muṟṟiṟṟu
   ----------------------
                     ___________________________________

                                 aṅkam - 2
                               mutaṟkaḷam

   iṭam:- muṟkūṟiya pūñcōlai
   kālam:- mālai
   pāttiram:- rūpāvati
   rūpāvati:- (taṉakkuḷ) malar koyyappōṉa ampujāṭciyuṅ kanakamālaiyum
   ivvaḷavu nēramākiyum ēṉ varavillai?
   (pāṭukiṉṟāḷ)
   * "miṉṉikarā mātē viraiccār tuṭaṉpuṇarntu
       niṉṉikarā mātavikka ṇiṉṟaruṇī--taṉṉikarāñ
       centī varamalaruñ ceṅkārtaṭ pōtuṭaṉē
       yintī varaṅkoṇartum yām" (15)

   eṉṟu kūṟiya taṅkaḷ vārttaiyai maṟantu viṭṭīrkaḷō?-
   (mauṉam) avviṭattil oru mayil kalāpam virittu āṭukiṉṟatu. ataip pōyp
   pārppēṉ; ataṟkuḷ ivarkaḷum varaṭṭum.
   [eḻuntu ciṟitutūram ulavukiṉṟāḷ.]
   iḵteṉṉa putumai! ivviṭattai mititta uṭaṉē eṉṉuṭaiya tēkam
   puḷakikkiṉṟatē! antō! itu vaṉṟō cuntarānantaṉ nēṟṟu irunta iṭam? iṅkut
   tāṉē avaṉ mūrccai pōy viḻuntatu? - eṉṉuṭaiya takappaṉārum ittuṇaik
   koṭiyarō? - avaṉuṭaiya uruvam eṉ kaṇṇukku etirē niṟkiṟatu pōlat
   tōṉṟukiṉṟatē! ō! cuntarānantaṉē! nēṟṟu uṉṉait taricittatu mutal uṉṉaip
   paricippatu ennāḷō ennāḷō eṉṟu ēṅki ēṅki varuntukiṉṟēṉ! nēṟṟirāmuḻutum
   tūkkameṉpatu ciṟitum varavillai! eḻuntu nilāmuṟṟattil ulāviṉēṉ! aiyō!
   koñcamēṉum karuṇai yeṉpatillāmaṟ cantiraṉ taṉatu nilavu ney peytu eṉatu
   kāmākkiniyai vaḷarttut tuṉpappaṭuttiṉāṉ! eṉṉa ceyvēṉ? mīṭṭum paṭuttuk
   koḷvēṉ! eḻuntiruppēṉ! ulāvuvēṉ! intappaṭi nēṟṟiravaik kaḻittēṉ. iṉṟu
   eṉṉa ceyvēṉō? - eṉṉuṭaiya ācaik kaṇṇāṉ cuntarānantaṉ oruvēḷai iṉṟaikkum
   varuvāṉō? vantāl yāṉ avaṉai iṉṉoru taramāvatu taricaṉam paṇṇi yāṟuvēṉ!
   ----------
   * taṇṭiyalaṅkāravurai
   (pāṭukiṉṟāḷ)
   tōkaimā mayilkā ḷeṉṉaic cuntarā nantaṉ vantāṟ
       kōkiyal nāḷka naṅkāḷ kūvumiṉ tayavu ceytē. (16)

   ----(mauṉam)
   eṉṉuṭaiya tōḻimār oruvēḷai itaik kēṭṭukkoṇṭu varuvārkaḷō? aiyō!
   eṉṉuṭaiya intac ceyalkaḷellām eṉ takappaṉārukkut terintu viṭṭāl eṉṉa
   ceyvēṉ! nāṉ keṭuvatē yaṉṟi eṉṉuṭaiya arumaic cuntaranantaṉukkum kēṭu
   viḷaiyumē! amma! ō! koṭumai! koṭumai!
   ---- atō ampujāṭci varukiṉṟāḷ. iṉimēl nāṉ itaik kuṟittu pēcappaṭātu
   ----
   [ ampujāṭci varukiṉṟāḷ.]
   ampujāṭci! ēṉ ivvaḷavu tāmatam?
   ampujāṭci:-- eṉṉavō? ammā! kanakamālaiyum nāṉum iraṇṭu pērumāyp pōṉōm.
   avaḷai yeṅkēyō kāṇēṉ. ituvaraikkum varuvaḷ varuvaḷeṉṟu kāttuk
   koṇṭiruntēṉ. iṉṉum varavillai. oruvēḷai eṉakku muntiyē iṅkē
   vantuviṭṭāḷō eṉṟu niṉaittu vantēṉ. avaḷ iṅkē vantāḷō?
   [kanakamālai varukiṉṟāḷ]
   rūpāvati:-- nī appaṭit tirumpippār yāreṉṟu!
   ampujāṭci:-- eṉṉaṭi ammā! kākamālai! ituvaraikkum eṅkē pōyiruntāy?
   uṉakkāka evvaḷavu nāḻikai kāttukkoṇṭirukkiṟatu? rūpāvati
   taṉiyāyirukkiṟāḷē eṉṟu vantuviṭṭēṉ.ētāvatu eṉ pērilē kōpamōṭi ammā?
   kanakamālai:-- itaṟkuk kōpameṉṉa kōpam? nī eṉṉa periya talai pōkiṟa
   kāriyañ ceytuviṭṭāy, ataṟku nāṉ uṉṉaik kōpittukkoḷḷa? eṉṉavō ippaṭiyē
   pōṉēṉ. aṅkē yōriṭattil iraṇṭu māṉkaḷ tuḷḷi viḷaiyāṭiṉa. anta
   vēṭikkaiyaip pārttukkoṇṭē iruntu viṭṭēṉ. atutāṉ koñcam
   tāmatamāyviṭṭatu. nammuṭaiya rūpāvatiyai vekunāḻikai kākkumpaṭi
   vaittuviṭṭōmē eṉṟu varuttappaṭukiṟēṉ.
   rūpāvati:-- inta aṟpa kāriyattiṟkāka nī varuttappaṭuvāṉēṉ? nāṉum iṅkē
   oru mayil aḻakāyt tōkai virittāṭiṟṟu. ataip pārttukkoṇṭiruntēṉ.
   ampujāṭci:-- ēṉ rūpāvati! nāṉ oṉṟu kēṭkiṟēṉ--kōpittuk koḷḷātiruntāṟ
   collukiṟēṉ.
   rūpāvati:-- kōpittuk koḷḷukiṟateṉṉa? collaṭi col.
   ampujāṭci:- appaṭi yoṉṟumillai. vēṭikkaiyāyttāṉ kēṭpō meṉṟu niṉaittēṉ.
   rūpāvati:- ataittāṉ iṉṉateṉṟu collu, kēṭpōm. cīkkiram collu.
   kanakamālai:- avaḷ eppoḻutum ippaṭittāṉ koñcam vīṇpiluk kellām
   paṇṇuvāḷ.
   ampujāṭci:- appaṭi ettaṉai nāḷ ammā uṉṉiṭattilnāṉ pilukkup paṇṇi
   yirukkiṟēṉ?
   rūpāvati:- veku! naṉṟāyirukkiṟatu aḵteṉṉai? nīyum ārampittu viṭṭāy!
   avaḷtāṉ coṉṉāṟ collaṭṭumē! avaḷukku uṉṉaic colla urimaiyillaiyā eṉṉa?
   ampujāṭci:- coṉṉāṟ collukiṟāḷ. ataṟkoṉṟumillai. nāṉ collavanta
   teṉṉaveṉṟu kēṭṭāl uṉ mukam eṉṉavō koñcam vāṭṭam aṭaintatu pōlak
   kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatē; aḵtu etaṉālē eṉṟu kēṭkattāṉ vāyeṭuttēṉ. ataṟkuḷḷē
   kanakamālai nāṉ pilukkup paṇṇuvatāka niṉaittuk koṇṭāḷ.
   rūpāvati:- atutāṉ avaḷ viḷaiyāṭṭāyc coṉṉāḷ; nīyēṉ atai maṟupaṭiyum
   maṟupaṭiyum tūṇṭic collukiṟāy? eṉṉavō viṭṭu vēṟē yētāvatu pēcu.
   kanakamālai:- ampujāṭci! nāṉ uṉṉait teriyātē colliviṭṭēṉ ammā! ivvaḷavu
   tūrañ colluvāyeṉṟu terintiruntāl nāṉ pēciyirukkavē māṭṭēṉ.
   rūpāvati:- cari, nīṅkaḷtām vātip pirativātikaḷām. nāṉ niyāyañ colvēṉām.
   eṅkē iraṇṭu pērum uṅkaḷ vaḻakkaic colluṅkaḷ. kēṭṭut tīrmāṉam colluvōm.
   ampujāṭci:- eṉṉaṭiyammā! nī kēlipaṇṇukiṟāyē yoḻiya nāṉ kēṭṭatai
   viṭṭuviṭṭāyē.
   rūpāvati:_ nī appaṭi eṉṉa kēṭṭāy? eṉakkoṉṟum ñāpakam illaiyē!
   ampujāṭci:- ñāpakamillaiyā? caritāṉ. nī eṅkaḷukkellām colluvaiyē ammā!
   rūpāvati:- nī kēṭṭatē yiṉṉateṉṟu terintālaṉṟō nāṉ collak kūṭum ammā!
   eṉpēriṟ kōpittuk koḷvatiṟ payaṉillai.
   ampujāṭci:- āṉāṟ collukiṟēṉ. iṉṉorutaram; kavaṉamāykkēḷu. uṉ mukattil
   tiṉamum irukkiṟa cantōṣakkaḷaiyai iṉṟaikku eṉṉavō kāṇōm! ataṉ
   kāraṇameṉṉa? collu.
   rūpāvati:- ataṉ kāraṇamā? - (mauṉam)
   kanakamālai:- eṉṉa yōcikkiṟāy? collu.
   ampujāṭci:- eṉṉa itu! aḻukiṟāyā? eṉṉa? ēṉ ammā! nāṅkaḷ iruvarum uṉ
   pakkattil uṉakkut tuṇaiyā yirukkiṟapōtu nī yaḻavēṇṭiya kāraṇameṉṉa?
   uṉṉai nāṅkaḷ yārum eṉṉavāvatu coṉṉōmā? allāmaṟ pōṉāl nāṅkaḷ ivvaḷavu
   nāḻikaiyā yuṉṉait taṉiyāy viṭṭuviṭṭup pōy viṭṭō meṉṟu
   vicaṉappaṭukiṟāyā? atuvumallāviṭṭāl evaiyāvatu mirukaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭu nī
   payantirukkalām eṉṟu colla etuvumillaiyē. eṉatu arumait talaiviyē!
   rūpāvati! nī ivvāṟu varuttappaṭuvatai nāṅkaḷ pārttuk koṇṭiruppōmō? uṉ
   maṉattilirukkiṟa tuṉpattai nī yeṅkaḷiṭattiṟ collat takātō?
   rūpāvati:- eṉakku ataic colla nāveḻavillai. (mauṉam)
   [kaṇṇīr vaṭikkiṟāḷ.]
   ampujāṭci:- aṭi! kanakamālai! nam rūpāvati meyyākavēyaḻukiṉṟāḷ. nī
   maṟṟait tōḻimāraiyum kūppiṭukiṟāyā? [iraṅkip pāṭukiṉṟāḷ.]
   karumpiḻi cāṟokkuṅ kaḷimōru yōṭu
       muruntiṉai māṉu muṟuvalum pūttu
       varuntā tiruntava ḷiṉṟō varunti
       yiruntā ḷiṟantavā kāṇīrē yeḻiluṭai yīrvantu kāṇīrē.(17)
       paṇkaḷi kūrntaṉi pāṭuṅ kuḻaviṉaṉ
       viṉko ḷamutamu mēvu mataratta
       ṇaṉpu koḷavaru nantamrū pāvati
       kaṇka ḷiruntaṉai kāṇīrē kārikai yīrvantu kāṇīrē. (18)

   kanakamālai:- ampujāṭci! intac cōlaiyilē oru vaṉatēvatai yuṇṭeṉṟu
   collak kēṭṭirukkiṟōmē. eṅkē atutāṉ oru vēḷai yivaḷ taṉiyāyirukkum pōtu
   vantu payamuṟuttiviṭṭup pōyiṟṟō? eṉṉavō?
   ampujāṭci:- eṉakku appaṭi yirukkumeṉṟu tōṉṟavillai. appaṭiyāyiṉ ippaṭic
   collamāṭṭāḷ. - ellāvaṟṟiṟkum, ēṉ rūpāvati! uḷḷa kāraṇattai eṅkaḷukku
   veḷiyiṭak kūṭātā? nāṅkaḷ uṉ tōḻikaḷallamō?
   [cuntarānantaṉ varukiṉṟāṉ.]
   rūpāvati:- (kaṇṇīrait tuṭaittukkoṇṭu) aḵtu oṉṟum avvaḷavu vicēṣamillai.
   nām pōkavēṇṭāmā? namakku nēramākavillaiyā?
   kanakamālai:-- tiṉamum varukiṟārkaḷē, anatap piḷḷaikaḷ iṉṟaikkum
   oruvēḷai vantālum varuvārkaḷ. caṟṟē iruntu vēṇṭumeṉṟāṟ pārttuviṭṭup
   pōvōm. ētāvatu vicaṉappaṭṭup pēcuvārkaḷ.kēṭpōm.
   rūpāvati:-- avarkaḷaip pārvai eṉṉa? kompu muḷaittirukkiṟatō?
   ampujāṭci:-- kanakamālaitāṉ collukiṟāḷē. iruntutāṉ pārttuviṭṭuppōvamē.
   eṉṉavō? eḷiyaṉ cuntarānantaṉ! avaṉai niṉaikkiṟapōtellām eṉakku
   vicaṉamāyirukkiṟatu. avaṉ talaiyilē piramā ippaṭik kaṣṭappaṭumpaṭi
   eḻutiviṭṭāṉ!
   rūpāvati:-- (vicaṉattai aṭakkikkoṇṭu) nēṟṟu aḻutu mūrccaipōy viḻuntāṉē!
   avaṉā?
   kanakamālai:-- āmām! avaṉtāṉ! aḻakāy iṉṉoruttaṉ maṭiyilē cāyntu
   koṇṭiruntavaṉ!
   rūpāvati:-- ēṇṭi! kanakamālai! nī ippōtu coṉṉāyē intac cuntarānantaṉ,
   amapujāṭci attai piḷḷai cukumāraṉ, ivarkaḷ iraṇṭu pērkaḷilum yār atika
   aḻakuṭāyavar?
   kanakamālai:-- cuntarānantaṉ tāṉ. collukiṟataṟkuk kēṭpāṉēṉ?
   [cukumāraṉ varukiṉṟāṉ.]
   [avaṉum cuntarānantaṉ irukkumiṭam vantu oḷintu niṟkiṉṟāṉ.]
   cuntarānantaṉ:--(maṟaivil) koñcam pēcātiru!
   rūpāvati:-- aracaṉ makaṉ eṉṟu collukiṟāyō?
   kanakamālai:-- eṉakkeṉṉaṭi ammā! munti rājāpiḷḷaiyā yiruntāl ippōteṉṉa
   ataṟku?
   rūpāvati:-- (taṉakkuḷ) eṉṉē ulakiyal! araciḻantārai yavamatittal
   naṉṟāyiruntatu! celvañ ciṟappō cemmai ciṟappō? (veḷiyāy) appuṟam,
   amapujāṭci caṇṭaikku varappōkiṟāḷ! cukumāraṉukkuc cuntarānantaṉuṭaiya
   aḻakillai eṉṟu collukiṟāyē!
   kanakamālai:-- ētammā! nīyē caṇṭaiyiḻuttu viṭuvāypōrukkiṟatē!
   ampujāṭci:-- eṉṉa! rūpāvati! ippaṭik kēli paṇṇukiṟāy!
   rūpāvati:-- cari, ivaiyellā mirukkaṭṭum. kanakamālai! nāṉ oṉṟu
   kēṭkiṟēṉ. colluvaiyā?
   kanakamālai:-- eṉṉa? kēḷu! collakkūṭiyatāyiruntāl collukiṉṟēṉ.
   rūpāvati:-- ampujāṭcitāṉ avaḷ attaipiḷḷai cukumāraṉukku vāḻkkaippaṭap
   pōkiṉṟāḷ. nī yārukku vāḻkkaippaṭap pōkiṉṟāy? cuntarānantaṉtāṉ
   aḻakāyirukkiṉṟāṉē1 nī ēṉ avaṉaik kalyāṇam ceytukoḷḷak kūṭātu?
   ampujāṭci:- appaṭic collappōṉāl, rūpāvati! uṉakkum avaṉukkuntāṉ īṭu
   cariyāyirukkiṟatu. uṉṉuṭaiya aḻakukku ēṟṟavaṉtāṉ avaṉum! avaṉuṭaiya
   aḻakukku ēṟṟavaḷtāṉ nīyum!
   rūpāvati:- (mukañcivantu) kanakamālaiyum eṉṉa? aḻakiṟkuṟaintavaḷō?
   avaḷum avaṉukku ēṟṟavaḷtāṉ! oruvēḷai avaṉ ippōtu ciṟaiccālaiyi
   lirukkiṟāṉē eṉṟu eṇṇuvāḷō eṉṉavō?
   kanakamālai:- avai yellām vīṇ pēccu. rūpāvati! uṉakku iṣṭamiruntāl nī
   avaṉukku vāḻkkaippaṭu.
   rūpāvati:- cari. ampujāṭci! kanakamālaikūṭa vīṇ pēccu eṉṟu collukiṉṟāḷ!
   nēramākiṟatu. vīṭṭiṟkup pōkavēṇṭum.
   ampujāṭci:- rūpāvati! nī oru pāṭṭup pāṭu.
   kanakamālai:- eṅkē rūpāvati! pāṭu.
   rūpāvati:- (pāṭukiṉṟāḷ)
   perumāṉvan tāṉeṉṟu pēcuvāy kiḷiyē
       pīru moḻintiṭap pēcuvāy kiḷiyē
       marumalar vīciṭap pēcuvāy kiḷiyē
       vantatu teṉṟalum pēcuvāy kiḷiyē (19)
       pōtaṉvan tāṉeṉṟu pēcuvāy kiḷiyē
       puṉkaṇmai tāṉeṉṟu pēcuvāy kiḷiyē
       nātaṉvan tāṉeṉṟu pēcuvāy kiḷiyē
       nāmu makiḻntiṭap pēcuvāy kiḷiyē. (20)
       maḻakaḷi ṟōṭiṭa varakuvāy mayilē
       māṭṭiṉaṉ vantāṉeṉ ṟāṭuvāy mayilē
       yaḻakiṉaṉ vantāṉeṉ ṟāṭuvāy mayilē
       yāṉantam vantateṉ ṟāṭuvāy mayilē. (21)
       vaḷḷalum vantāṉeṉ ṟāṭuvāy mayilē
       mataṉvātai nīṅkiṟṟeṉ ṟāṭuvāy mayilē
       viḷḷa malarmaṇa māṭuvāy mayilē
       vīcum poḻilakat tāṭuvāy mayilē. (22)
       talaivaṉvan tāṉeṉṟu kūvuvāy kuyilē
       taṉṉai maṟantiṭak kūvuvāy kuyilē
       kalaiñaṉvan tāṉeṉṟu kūvuvāy kuyilē
       kaḷimikun tāṭiṭak kūvuvāy kuyilē. (23)
       iṟaivaṉvan tāṉeṉṟu kūvuvāy kuyilē
       yeṉṉai yaṇaintiṭak kūvuvāy kuyilē
       ciṟaiyiṉai nīṅkiyē kūvuvāy kuyilē
       cēra maṉatteṇṇik kūvuvāy kuyilē. (24)

   ampujāṭci:- pāṭṭu veku cukamā yirukkiṟatu.
   kanakamālai:- iṉṟaikku nām vantu veku nāḻikaiyāy viṭṭatu. ākaiyiṉālē
   nām cīkkiram pōkavēṇṭum ammā!
   rūpāvati:-cari. ēṉ ampujam! pōvamē!
   ampujāṭci:- kuṟaippeyarāyk kūppiṭṭāyāṉā luṉakkuk kuṟaippiḷḷaitāṉ
   piṟakkum! pārttukkoḷ.
   rūpāvati:- appaṭiyē piṟantālum oṉṟutāṉē yappaṭippiṟakkum. pōkaṭṭum.
   maṟṟaip piḷḷaikaḷāvatu naṉṟāyp piṟakkumē yallavō? atu pōtum.
   ampujāṭci:- ēṉ? tāmatam eṉṉa? pōkalāmē.
   rūpāvati:- tāmatamoṉṟumillai. pōkalām. ampujāṭci nī iṉṟiravu māttiram
   eṅkaḷ araṇmaṉaiyil eṉṉōṭu taṅkiyiruntu viṭiyaṟkālam nīyuṉ vīṭṭiṟkup
   pōkalāmē.
   ampujāṭci:- appaṭiyāṉāl nāṉippōtu vīṭṭiṟkup pōy eṉ takappaṉāriṭam
   anumati peṟṟukkoṇṭu vantuviṭukiṟēṉ.
   [rūpāvati, ampujāṭci, kanakamālai pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
   cukumāraṉ:- (veḷivantu) nī vantu veku nāḻikaiyāyviṭṭatō! ēṉ
   cuntarānantā! ippōtu pēcikkoṇ ṭiruntārkaḷē, inta mūṉṟu peṇkaḷum iṉṉar
   eṉpatu uṉakkut teriyumō?
   cuntarānantaṉ:- mantiri cucīlar peṇ orutti; avaḷtāṉ uṉ māmā peṇ. atu
   niccayamāyt terikiṉṟatu. maṟṟaiyiraṇṭu pērum yārō? naṉṟāyt
   teriyavillai.
   cukumāraṉ:- eṉṉuṭaiya māmā peṇ peyar ampujāṭci.- maṟṟa iraṇṭu
   pērkaḷilum atika aḻakuḷḷavaḷā yiruntāḷē avaḷ tāṉ uṉṉai yikkōlamākkiya
   cūracēnaṉ putalvi. avaḷukku avaḷuṭaiya aḻakukkut takkapaṭi 'rūpāvati'
   yeṉṟu peyariṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. maṟṟavaḷ 'kanakamālai' yeṉṉum
   peyaruṭaiyavaḷ. atait tavira vēṟoṉṟu mavaḷaippaṟṟi eṉakkut teriyātu.
   cuntarānantaṉ:- eṉṉa! cantiramukaṉ iṉṟu ituvaraiyilum varavillai.
   ētāvatu avaṉ vīṭṭil iṉṟaikku vicēṣamuṇṭō? avaṉ orunāḷum ivvaḷavu
   tāmatam ceyyamāṭṭāṉē!
   cukumāraṉ:- ataṟkeṉṉa ippōtu? avaṉ meḷḷattāṉ varaṭṭumē!
   cuntarānantaṉ:- ataippaṟṟi yoṉṟumillai. avaṉ cīkkiram varukiṟavaṉē eṉṟu
   coṉṉēṉ.
   cukumāraṉ:- ō! cuntarānantā! nī yiṉṟu mālaippoḻutai yevvāṟu kaḻittāy?
   ētēṉum paṭittāyō?
   cuntarānantaṉ:- paṭittilēṉ. āyiṉum em ālavāypperumāṉai vāyārap pāṭi
   tutittēṉ. atuvē yeṉ maṉattiṟ ciṟitu amaiti viḷaittatu. atait tavirttu
   vēṟoṉṟuñ ceytilēṉ.
   cukumāraṉ:- nī pāṭit tutittatai nāṉ kēṭkalāmō? kēṭkalāmeṉiṉ ataic col.
   cuntarānantaṉ:-(pāṭukiṉṟāṉ)
   cemmai yuḷḷa citānanta ṉēyuṉṟa
       ṉaṉmai yāṉa navilarun taṉmaiyiṟ
       kimmai yillaṭi yēmic ciṟaiyiṉi
       lamma vōveṉ ṟaraṟṟuta lākumō? (25)
       vaiya mēttuṟu māmatu rāpurit
       tuyya ṉēmati cūṭiya cuntarā
       ceyya niṉṉaruṭ cepparun taṉmaiyiṟ
       kaiya kōveṉ ṟalaṟuta ṉītiyō? (26)
       āṉēṟiṉi tēṟīyeḷai yāḷummatu rēcā
       tēṉeyeṉuñ collāḷumai cērummiṭap pākā
       kāṉēykuḻaṟ kaṅkātara niṉpēraru ḷiṟkē
       yāṉēyeṉak kōṭṭattili ṇaintēyeḻa lāmō? (27)
       campō cāmpacivā caka mellām paṭaittavaṉē
       vampār pūmpoḻilcūḻ matu rāpuri vāḻpavaṉē
       yampā vukkuṭali loru pāti yaḷittavaṉē
       yempā ṉīyirakka miṉi yēṉum purintaruḷē. (28)
       mūvā muṉṉavaṉē mutalākiya mukkaṇaṉē
       kōvē kōmaḷaṉē kuḷir cantira cēkaraṉē
       nāvāṟ pāṭukiṉṟē nala muḷḷa maturaiyaṉē
       tēvā nīkaruṇai ciṟi tēṉum purintaruḷē. (29)

   cukumāraṉ:-ō! cuntarānantā! nī pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ mikavum
   naṉṟayirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivai yellām pūrvajaṉma puṇṇiyam!
   cuntarānantaṉ:-(kaṇṇīr tatumpa) jaṉmāntarattil yāṉ puṇṇiyamuñ ceytēṉō?
   appā! cukumārā! nī yaṟiyātu kūṟukiṉṟāy! yāṉ pūrvajaṉmattiṟ puṇṇiyañ
   ceytiruppēṉāyiṉ ippōtu ēṉ intavitat tuṉpamellām anupavikkavēṇṭum? ivai
   yellām eṉṉuṭaiya jaṉmāntara pāvattiṉālēyā meṉpatu tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
   cukumāraṉ:- cari. inta eṇṇattiṟku, ippoḻutu iṭaṅ koṭuttōmō nēṟṟaippōla
   ētēṉum viparītam viḷaikkum. appuṟam mikka tuṉpam varum. ākaiyāl vēṟu
   viṣayattaik kuṟittup pēcuvōm.
   cuntarānantaṉ:- iṉṟaikku ētāvatu vicēṣam irukkattāṉ vēṇṭum. illāviṭiṟ
   cantiramukaṉ ivvaḷavu tāmatam ceyya māṭṭāṉ.
   cukumāraṉ:- avaṉum varukiṟa camayantāṉē! vantuviṭuvāṉ. itu kiṭakkaṭṭum.
   atu mūṉṟu peṇkaḷum pēcik koṇṭatiliruntu oṉṟu paṭukiṉṟatu.
   cuntarānantaṉ:- aḵteṉṉai? eṉakkoṉṟum tōṉṟavillaiyē! eṅkē? aḵtu
   iṉṉateṉṟu collu. kēṭpōm.
   cukumāraṉ:- atuvā? collukiṉṟēṉ kēḷ. aracaṉ puttiri rūpāvatikku uṉ pēriṟ
   koñcam piriyamirukkiṟatu eṉpatu kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu.
   cuntarānantaṉ:- aḵteppaṭik kaṇṭāy? veku naṉṟāyirukkiṉṟatē!
   cukumāraṉ:- avarkaḷ pēcikkoṇṭirunta campāṣaṇaiyiliruntu nāṉ anumāṉittuk
   koṇṭēṉ.
   cuntarānantaṉ:- appaṭiyāṉāl eṉakkēṉ tōṉṟavillai?
   cukumāraṉ:- aḵteṉṉaiyō? yāṉaṟiyēṉ. eṉ maṉattil tōṉṟiṉataic coṉṉēṉ.
   ataittavira vēṟillai.
   cuntarānantaṉ:- āyiṉ amaiyum. ovvoruvarun tamatu maṉattil tamakku
   vēṇṭiyaṉavaṟṟai yellām niṉaittuk koḷḷalām. – āyiṉum eṉakkut tōṉṟukiṟa
   maṭṭil anta mūṉṟu peṇkaḷum nām nēṟṟaip pēcik koṇṭirunta caṅkatikaḷai
   yoḷittuk kēṭṭiruppārkaḷō veṉpatu tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ēṉeṉṟāl eṉṉaikkuṟitta
   rūpāvati 'nēṟṟu mūrccai pōy viḻuntāṉē, avaṉā?' eṉṟu kēṭṭāḷ.
   cukumāraṉ:- āṉāl veku nallatāyiṟṟē!
   cuntarānantaṉ:- aḵteppaṭi yappā?
   cukumāraṉ:- uṉ pēril irakkaṅkoṇṭu oru vēḷai taṉ takappaṉāraik kēṭṭu
   uṉṉaic ciṟaiccālaiyi ṉiṉṟum viṭṭuviṭac collak kūṭum. ataṉālētāṉ
   nallateṉṟēṉ.
   cuntarānantaṉ:- appaṭittāṉ avaḷ ceyvāleṉpateṉṉai? oru vēḷai yeṉṉuṭaya
   caṅkatikaḷai yellān taṉ takappaṉāriṭañ colli yeṉṉai yiṉṉum atikamāṉa
   tuṉpaṅkaḷukku ēṉ
   uṭpaṭutta māṭṭāḷ? appaṭic ceytālum ceyyak kūṭumē?
   cukumāraṉ:- nāṉ avaḷaippaṟṟik kēḷvippaṭṭa maṭṭil avaḷ nallavaḷē eṉpatu
   tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ākaiyāl nī niṉaikkiṟapaṭi avaḷ ceyyamāṭṭāḷ eṉpataṟku
   ākṣēpamē yillai.
   cuntarānantaṉ:- appaṭiyāṉāl eṉ ālavāyt tēvaṉai yāṉ pāṭit tutittatu
   palittuviṭṭateṉṟu collalām. appā! atō! aṅkē varukiṉṟāṉ cantiramukaṉ.
   [cantiramukaṉ varukiṉṟāṉ.]
   cukumāraṉ:- eṅkē?
   cantiramukaṉ:- vantaṉam cuntarānantā!
   cukumāraṉ:- ēṉ, cantiramukā! uṉṉai evvaḷavu kālam etirpārttuk
   koṇṭiruppō meṉṟu niṉaittāy?
   cantiramukaṉ:- nāṉ uṉ vīṭṭiṟpōy uṉṉait tēṭiṉēṉ. avviṭattilē nī
   yillāmaiyāl iṅkē tāṉ vantiruppā yeṉṟu niṉaittu vantēṉ. atutāṉ
   nāḻikaiyāyviṭṭatu. akkuṟṟattaip poṟuppīrkaḷeṉṟu niṉaikkiṉṟēṉ. mēlum nāṉ
   tāmatittu vantataṟku vēṟu kāraṇamum uṇṭu. aḵteṉṉeṉṟāl: eṉṉuṭaiya
   tantaiyār eṉṉai yaḻaittu irakaciya camācāra moṉṟu colli, ataippaṟṟi
   uṉṉiṭattil terivikkumpaṭi āṇaiyiṭṭaṉuppiṉār.
   cuntarānantaṉ:- aḵteṉṉa appaṭi yirakaciyamāṉa camācāram?
   cukumāraṉ:- ō! cuntarānantā! nī yeṇṇiyapaṭi, cantiramukaṉum ētō vicēṣa
   camācāram koṇṭuvan tirukkiṉṟāṉē!
   cuntarānantaṉ:- appaṭi yallāviṭṭāl avaṉ kālakaraṇam ceyyak
   kāraṇamillaiyē! cari cantiramukā! camācāra miṉṉateṉṟu col. kēṭpōm.
   cantiramukaṉ:- iṉṟu eṉṉuṭaiya vīṭṭil iraṇṭu mūṉṟu kaṉavāṉkaḷ vantu
   eṉṉuṭaiya tantaiyāroṭu cilavaṟṟaik kuṟittup pēciṉārkaḷ. avai
   yeṉṉaveṉṟāl, uṉṉai iccūracēnaṉ ciṟaiyilaṭaittiruppatum, uṉ peṟṟōrai
   valliyam palatirivaṉattut turattiyatum, niyāyamalla veṉpatum ivvita
   aniyāyattai niṟuttuvataṟkut takka yuktikaḷ yāvaiyō avaṟṟiṉ nāṭi muyaṟci
   ceyyavēṇṭu meṉpatuvēyām. itu viṣayattil uṉ yōcaṉai yeṉṉaveṉṟu eṉ
   tantaiyār kēṭṭuvarumpaṭi eṉṉiṭañ coṉṉār.
   cuntarānantaṉ:- ak kaṉavāṉkaḷ eṇṇiya yōcaṉaikaḷellām nallaṉavēyām.
   āyiṉum cūracēnaṉ eṉṉaiyum eṉ peṟṟōraiyum kolaikkaḷam paṭātu
   viṭṭiruttalē pērutaviyām. ākavē yittaṉmaiyāṉa upakārikku virōtamāka
   ētēṉuñ ceyya muyalutal ceynnaṉṟi kōṟalām.
   * "naṉṟi maṟappatu naṉṟaṉṟu naṉṟalla
       taṉṟē maṟappatu naṉṟu" (30)

   eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟiyiruttalaip palamuṟaiyum namatu āciriyar
   vittiyācākarap pulavar eṭutturait tirukkiṉṟaṉarē! aḵtuṇarntiruntum nī
   yivvāṟu kūṟutal nītiyākumō?
   ------------
   * tirukkuṟaḷ
   cukumāraṉ:- appā! cuntarānantā! nī kūṟuvatu mikavum naṉṟu! naṉṟu! inta
   makāvañcakaṉ cūracēnaṉ viṣayattil nītiyeṉpatē pārtta loṇṇātu.
   tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār kūṟuvaṉavellām nalloḻukka muṭaiyavarkaṭkēyām. tīyoḻukka
   muṭaiyavarkaṭku nītiyētu? niyāyamētu? tarumamētu?
   cuntarānantaṉ:- ō, cukumārā! nāṉ colliyatai maṟantuviṭṭu nī yivvāṟu
   kūṟalāmō? cūracēnaṉ tarumacintai yuṭaiyavaṉ eṉpataṟku ākṣēpamumuṇṭō?
   eṉṉaik kollātu viṭṭiruppatē avaṉuṭaiya nalla maṉattiṟkut takkacāṉṟu
   pakarum.
   cukumāraṉ:- kolluvatu pāvam, ākavē kollāmai pāvamillaiyē
   yaṉṟi atu puṇṇiyamākumō? iṅṅaṉaṅ kollā tiruttalōṭu oruvaṉ etaik
   kollātirukkiṉṟāṉō ataṟku utaviyum naṉmaiyuñ ceyvāṉāṉāl atu puṇṇiyam.
   maṟṟaippaṭi kollā tiruttal orunāḷum puṇṇiya mākātu.
   cuntarānantaṉ:- ō, cukumārā! iṉṉuṅ kēṭpāy! eṉṉaik kollā tiruttalēyaṉṟi
   yeṉṉaic ciṟaiccālaiyi ṉiṉṟum veḷiyē pōyulāvutaṟkum eṉatu arumai
   naṇparkaḷākiya uṅkaḷuṭaṉ pēcik kaḷippataṟkum uttaravu koṭuttiruttal
   uṉakkut teriyātō?
   cantiramukaṉ:- (taṉakkuḷ) naṭuvil nāmoṉṟuñ collakkūṭātu.
   cukumāraṉ:- iṉṉoruviṣaya muṉakkut teriyātē! intak kolaiñaṉ uṉṉaic
   ciṟaiccālaiyilē yiṭavēṇṭumeṉṟum, veḷiyēpōka viṭakkūṭāteṉṟum, collittāṉ
   mutalmutalil uttaravu ceytāṉām. piṟaku eṉṉuṭaiya māmaṉār cucīlar
   aracaṉai nōkki nīr appaṭic ceyvīrāṉāl jaṉaṅkaḷ ummaik koṭiyavareṉṟu
   niṉaikkakkūṭum eṉṟu coṉṉataik kēṭṭapiṟpāṭu tāṉ ippaṭik kaṭṭaḷai
   yiṭṭāṉām.
   cuntarānantaṉ:- irukkaṭṭum. ellāvaṟṟiṟkum cantiramukaṉai muṟṟiluṅ
   kēṭpōm. ēṉ cantiramukā! aracaṉ koṭumaiyai niṟuttuvataṟku ak kaṉavāṉkaḷ
   ētēṉum yukti ceytirukkkiṉṟārkaḷāmē?
   cantiramukaṉ:- ām. appaṭittāṉ coṉṉār. aḵteṉṉeṉṟāl: oru kaṉavāṉ aracaṉ
   vītiyil ulāvarumpōtu avaṉaik kuttip pōṭuvatākac coṉṉārām. maṟṟaik
   kaṉavāṉkaḷum, atu cariyeṉṟu anumōtittārkaḷām. āṉāl, eṉ tantaiyār
   māttiram pataṟakkūṭāteṉṟum, itaikkuṟittu iṉṉum yōcaṉai ceyyavēṇṭu
   meṉṟum coṉṉārām. itai yoruvar ottukkoṇṭārām. maṟṟai yiruvarum
   ottukkoḷḷa villaiyām. ataṉpēril itaikkuṟittu uṉṉiṭattil teriyap
   paṭutti, itaṟku nī yeṉṉa collukiṉṟāyō ataṉpaṭi naṭappatāka ellōrum
   tīrmāṉittārkaḷām. avvaḷavutāṉ. nī yeṉṉa collac collukiṉṟāyō atai yeṉ
   tantaiyāriṭañ collukiṉṟēṉ.
   cuntarānantaṉ:- ētappā! mika vintaiyā yirukkiṉṟatē! oṉṟum payaṉpaṭum
   yuktiyāyk kāṇappaṭa villaiyē! itaik kuṟittuc colluvataṟku eṉak koṉṟun
   tōṉṟavillai. ākaiyāl uṉ tantaiyār nayavacanariṭam eṉ vantaṉaṅkaḷaic
   colli avar iṣṭam eppaṭiyō appaṭiyē naṭakkumpaṭi coṉṉēṉ eṉṟu collu,
   cantiramukā!
   cukumāraṉ:- cari. appuṟam cantiramukā! uṉakkut teriyātēyoru caṅkati!
   nēṟṟuc cuntarānantaṉ mūrccaipōy viḻuntatu mutal nām pēcikkoṇṭa
   camācāramellā vaṟṟaiyum aracaṉ puttiri rūpāvati, eṉ māmā peṇ ampujāṭci,
   kanakamālai yinta mūṉṟu pērum oṟṟuk kēṭṭuk koṇṭiruntirukkiṟārkaḷ. nām
   ataip pārkkavillai. iṉṟaikku nāṉum cuntarānantaṉum avarkaḷ pēcik
   koṇṭatai yoḷittiruntu kēṭṭōm. atutāṉ eṅkaḷukkut teriyum.
   cantiramukaṉ:- appaṭiyā! ateppaṭik kēṭpārkaḷappā! āccariyamā
   yirukkiṉṟatē!
   cukumāraṉ:- ateṉṉavō! teriyātappā! vēṭikkaitāṉ!
   cuntarānantaṉ:- ēṉ cantiramukā! avarkaḷ kēṭṭataṉālē ētāvatu keṭutal
   varumō? nāṉ aracaṉaik koñcam varuttattiṉāl vaitēṉē!
   cukumāraṉ:- nāṉtāṉ coṉṉēṉē! nallatē yoḻiyakkeṭutalillai. rūpāvatitāṉ
   atika irakkamuḷḷavaḷē!
   cantiramukaṉ;- āmām! eṉakkum appaṭittāṉ tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. avaḷ
   aḻakukkellaiyum aṉpukkākaramumāvaḷē!
   cuntarānantaṉ:- appaṭiyāyiṉ nallatutāṉ. nīṅkaḷ collukiṟatum, nāṉ
   collukiṟatu mellām iṉimēl teriyum.
   cukumāraṉ:- itu nūṟṟilorupēccu! pārppamē!
   [yāvarum pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
   ------------------------
                     ___________________________________

                     aṅkam - 2 / iraṇṭāṅkaḷam

   iṭam:- irācaputtiriyiṉ kaṉṉimāṭam
   kālam:- naḷḷiravu
   pāttiram:- rūpāvati
   [ampujāṭci yorupuṟam tuyilpurikiṉṟāḷ.]
   rūpāvati:- (taṉakkuḷ) aḻakum iṉpamum aṉpum niṟaintacuntarānantaṉē!
   cuntarānantaṉē! ikkuṇaṅkaḷ uṉṉiṭattil iruppatai aṟintu tāṉō uṉṉai
   yuṉpeṟṟōrkaḷ 'cuntarānantaṉ' eṉṟu aḻaittārkaḷ? aiyō! uṉṉuṭaiya
   malarntavataṉattaiyum matura vacaṉattaiyuṅ kaṇṭuṅ kēṭṭum
   āṉantappaṭuvatu eṉṟaikkō eṉṟaikkō eṉṟu nāṉ uṉṉai niṉaittu ēṅkuvatai
   nīyaṟiyāyō? eṉ takappaṉār uṉṉai ciṟaiyiliṭṭiruppataṉāl varuntukiṉṟāyō?
   eṉ takappaṉār koṭiyavarāyiruttal paṟṟi nāṉuṅ koṭiyavaḷā yiruppēṉeṉṟu
   eṇṇuvāyō? antō! aracamarattaip piṭitta caṉiyaṉ ataṉkaṇ amarnta
   piḷḷaiyāraiyum piṭittatāmeṉṟu ulakōr kūṟum vacaṉampōla avar koṭumai
   eṉṉiṭattum irukkumeṉṟu cantēkippāyō? uṉṉai mutalmutaliṟ kaṇṭatutoṭṭu
   eṉmaṉam eṉṉiṭattilēyē yirāmal uṉ mukāravintattaiyē pārttut taṉṉaiyum
   eṉṉaiyum maṟantu mayaṅki niṟkiṉṟatu kaṇṭilaiyō? accō! cuntaraṉē!
   āṉantaṉē! cuntarānantaṉē! uṉ mukamatiyiṉ amirtattai yuṇṭu uyir tarippa
   tennāḷō?---
   (mauṉam)
   ō! cakōraṅkaḷē! nīvirellām cantira kiraṇaṅkaḷai kaḷippuṭaṉuṇṭu cerukkit
   tirikiṉṟīrkaḷē! umakkuḷ eṉṉaippōl āṟṟātu ēṅkiyiraṅkuvā rillaiyō?
   ammavō! maṉmataṉ kuyiṟkāḷa mūtukiṉṟāṉē! iṉi yeṅṅaṉamōṭi yoḷippēṉ?
   intappāvi maṉmataṉ uruviliyāyirukkum pōtē arivaiyarkaḷai ippāṭu
   paṭuttukiṉṟāṉē! uruvōṭu kūṭiyiruppāṉāyiṉ eṉpōliyar piḻaittaluṅ kūṭumō?
   appavō! iṉṟu mālai eṉṉuyir nilaiyākiya cuntarānanta mūrttiyait
   taricikkum eṇṇattōṭu pōyiṉēṉē! appaṭip pōyum eṉatarumait talaivaṉai
   maṟupaṭiyuṅ kāṇṭaṟkillātu pōyiṟṟē! pōyiṟṟē!! iṉiyeṉṉa ceyvēṉ?
   evvitamāṟuvēṉ? nāṉ pūṅkāvaṉattiliruntapōtu eṉṉuṭaiya tōḻiyarēṉum iṉṉuñ
   ciṟitu atika nēraṅ kaḻittu vantārillaiyē!-(mauṉam) ō!mātavi maramē! nī
   ceyta puṇṇiyam nāṉ ceytilaṉē! nīyuṉ ṉaṭiyil eṉ piriyanāyakaṉai
   vaittiruntaṉaiyē! nāṉ avaṉai yippōtu kāṇap peṟuvēṉē avaṉai eṉtalaiyil
   vaittuk koṇṭaṉṟō kaḷikkūttāṭuvēṉ!-(mauṉam) ō! kaṇkāḷ! nīvirēṉumoru
   muṟai yeṉatu talaivaṉait taricittīr! cevikāḷ! nīvir orutaram avaṉ pēcak
   kēṭṭīr! ātaliṉ nīvir pākkiyavāṉkaḷē yāvīr! uṅkaḷ pākkiyam eṉatu maṟṟaip
   poṟikaḷukkuṅ kiṭaikka villaiyē! avai nummāṭṭup pōṟāmaiyāṟ puḻuṅki
   yēṅkukiṉṟaṉavē! yāṉē yivvāṟu varuntā niṟka, avaṟṟai āṟṟuvār yāvar?
   aiyavō! karuṇaiyillākkāmaṉum iruḷ yāṉai muṉṉēlit teṉṟalun tērēṟip
   pōrkkaḷattup pukkaṉaṉē!
   eṉṟaṉatu kātalaṉē yivviṭattu vārāyō
       maṉṟavē yeṉṟuyara māṟṟāyō-kuṉṟaṉaiya
       tōḷuṭaiya kōmakaṉē cuntarā nantaveḻiṟ
       kāḷaiyē yeṉṉilaimai kaṇṭu. (31)
       māraṉ malarkkaṇai mārpiṉiṟ pāyntu mayaṅkiyinta
       nēra muṉaiyē niṉaintunon tēṉeṉṟa ḷīḷaṉaka
       pāram poṟuttila ṉeṉcevva leṉpāṟ pariviṉōṭum
       pūraṇa vaṉpuṟu cuntarā kantap puravalaṉē. (32)

   ampujāṭci:- (kaṇ viḻittukkoṇṭu) eṉṉa? ammā! rūpāvati! nīyaṅkē nilā
   muṟṟattil eṉṉa ceytu koṇṭirukkiṉṟāy?
   rūpāvati:- ampujāṭci! nī yeṉṉa paṭuttukkoṇṭavuṭaṉē yuṟaṅki viṭṭaṉaiyē!
   eṉakku ituvaraikkun tūkkamē varavillai. nāṉ tūkkam varukiṟataṟkāka
   evvaḷavō muyaṉṟu pārttum varavillai.
   aḵtirukkaṭṭum.
   (ampujāṭci yeḻukiṉṟāḷ.)
   ampujāṭci! atō pārttāyā? vaikai yāṟṟiṉ kaṇ veḷḷam pōkiṉṟatu!
   ampujāṭci:- āmām. nām poḻutuviṭintatum putuppuṉalāṭak kaṉaka
   mālaiyaiyuṅ kūṭṭikkoṇṭu maṟṟait tōḻimārōṭum pōvōm.
   rūpāvati:- putuppuṉalāṭuta lirukkaṭṭum. maṟṟaippaṭi nīyanta natiyaip
   pār! cantira kiraṇaṅkaḷ taṇṇīriṟ paṭuvataṉālē atu paḷa paḷaveṉṟu
   miṉṉukiṉṟatu! eṅkē! ampujāṭci nī vittiyācākarap pulavar ceytaṉaveṉṟu
   cila yāṟṟuvarip pāṭṭukkaḷ pāṭuvāyē! avaṟṟiloṉṟupāṭu. kēṭpōm.
   ampujāṭci:- (pāṭukiṉṟāḷ)
   māṟṟuvari
       iṉpa vaṉappu mikavuṭaiyā ṉiṉitu ceṅkō latukōccat
       tuṉpa moḻita luṇṭukolō colvāy vāḻi vaiyaiyē
       tuṉpa moḻita luṇṭukolō collā toḻita ṟuṭiyiṭaiyā
       yaṉpi lāmai yeṉṟaṉṟō vaṟivēṉ vāḻi vaiyaiyē. (33)

   rūpāvati! nīyum ētāvatu vēṟu varippāṭṭuppāṭu.
   rūpāvati:- (pāṭukiṉṟāḷ)
   murivari
       oḻukuṟu niṇamuṭaiya vorutaṉi vēlavaṉu
       muḻumati yuṟaḻmukaṉu mukamalar vuṭaṉuṟaiyap
       paḻutaṟu tamiḻmoḻiyā laḻutaṉai puviyumeṉai
       yeḻiluṟu miṉiyavaṉē yevaḷiṭar ceytaṉaiyē. (34)
       cāyalvari
       cōlai taṉṉiṟ ṟuḷiyuṟ ṟēṅki
       mālai varuntic ceṉṟā ṉoruvaṉ
       mālai varuntic ceṉṟā ṉavaṉeṉ
       māṟu maṉamviṭ ṭakalvā ṉallaṉ. (35)
       nilaivari
       vaṉappuṭaiyā ṉiṉpuṭaiyāṉ vaḻutiyartaṅ kōmāṉ
       maṉattiliṉilē nōyviṉaittāṉ māraṉō kāṇīr
       māraṉō nāṇīr vaṉamaturai yiṟṟeṉṟaṟ
       ṟēriṉaiyē nīṅkit tirikiṉṟa kāvalaṉē. (36)

   antō! paṭukkaiyaṟaiyaiviṭṭuvantu vekunēramāy viṭṭatē! ampujāṭci!
   cevilittāy vantu nampaṭukkai vēṟuvitamāyiruppataip pārttāl eṉṉa
   colvāḷō? iṉiyivviṭattilirukkap paṭātu.
   [iruvarum pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
   -------------------
                     ___________________________________

                     aṅkam - 2 / mūṉṟāṅkaḷam

   iṭam:- ciṟaiccālai
   kālam:- naḷḷiravu
   pāttiram:- cuntarānantaṉ
   cuntarānantaṉ:- eṉṉuṭaiya tantaiyun tāyum innēram eṅkirukkiṟārkaḷō?
   ippōtiravāyiṟṟē! iravil tīyavilaṅkukaḷ vaṉattilulāvumē! avaṟṟiṉiṉṟum
   avarkaḷ evvāṟu tappiṉarō? avarkaḷ uyirōṭiruntālaṉṟō cakalamum iṉimaiyāy
   muṭiyum? illaiyāyiṉ iṉpameṉpatētu? vāḻkkaiyeṉpatētu?
   (mauṉam) iṉṟu nayavacanar tamatu puttiraṉ cantiramukaṉ mūlamāyccolli
   yaṉuppiya viṣayam mikappayaṅkaramā yirukkiṉṟatē! nayavacakar verunāḷai
   yanupavamuḷḷavarātaliṉ avviṣayattil avaratu iṣṭappaṭiyē avarceyyaṭṭum!
   yāṉ intak kāriyaṅkaḷilellān talaiyiṭutal matiyīṉamēyām. – ellāvaṟṟiṟkum
   nāḷaip pūñcōlaikkaṇ- 'pūñcōlai' yeṉṟavuṭaṉ eṉmaṉam pūrikkiṉṟatē!
   uṭalcilirkkiṉṟatē! ā! rūpāvati! rūpāvati! uṉṉaip pārttatumutal yāṉ,
   putumaiyāṉatōr maṉōvikārattai yaṭaintēṉ! ataiyeṉṉeṉṟu eṭutturaippēṉ? nī
   aḻakiṉukku ellaiyāyuḷḷaṉai yeṉpatupaṟṟi 'rūpāvati' eṉṟu niṉakkup
   peyariṭṭārkaḷpōlum! ō! nalleḻil peṟṟa naṅkai nāyakamē! aṟivu niṟai
   orppuk kaṭaippiṭiyeṉṟu ciṟappittuc collappaṭum āṭavar kuṇam nāṉkum
   puṉalōṭuvaḻip puṟcāyntāṟpōla niṉṉaikkaṇṭavuṭaṉ! cāyntuviṭṭaṉa! niṉṉaik
   kāṇṭalum kāmuṟṟēṉ! atu mutal yāṉ niṉṉaiyiṉṟiyamaiyātava ṉāyiṉēṉ!
   aṅṅaṉam uṉṉaiyiṉṟiyamaiyātu niṉṟavēṭkaiyāṉatu eṉatu ellā uṇarviṉaiyum
   nīkkit tāṉēyāy nāṇvaḻikkācupōlavum, nīr vaḻi mitavaipōlavum eṉṉai
   yiḻāniṉṟatu! nīyum eṉmītu kātal koṇṭirukkiṉṟā yeṉṟu nampuvataṟkuc
   ciṟitu iṭamuḷatu. āyiṉum ataṉai yāṉ evvāṟu niccayamāy nampuvaṉō? nī yuṉ
   pāṅkimāruṭaṉ pēcikkoṇṭatiliruntum uṉakku eṉmītiṟ ciṟitu iyaṟkai
   yaṉpuḷateṉṟu kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉ. atupaṟṟi yāṉ uṉṉiṭattil evvāṟu kātal
   kūṟuvēṉ? aiyō! yāṉ uṉṉai yeṉṉuyirt tuṇaiyāyp peṟa niṉaippatu muṭavaṉ
   komputtēṉai yiccittu ataṉai yavaṉpeṟa niṉaippatu pōlum! uṉ tantaiyō
   eṉṉai eppoḻutu kolaikkaḷam paṭukkalā meṉṟu yōciyā niṟkiṉṟāṉ! yāṉō
   uṉṉaik kaippiṭittu eṉatu talaiviyāyk koḷvatu eppaṭiyō eṉṟu yōciyā
   niṟkiṉṟēṉ! yāṉ uṉṉaiyeṉatu pirāṇa nāyakiyāyp peṟavēṇṭu meṉṟāṟ
   cuntaramūrttināyaṉār paravai nācciyāraip peṟṟatu pōlat tāṉ peṟa vēṇṭum!
   vēṟu vakaiyillai. teyvantā ṉutaviceyya vēṇṭum ivviṣayattil.
   (mauṉam)
   [pāṭukiṉṟāṉ]
   (2) irākam - ceñcuruṭṭi. tāḷam - āti
       pallavi
       uṉṉaiyē ṉāyaki yāyuṟa lākumō
       uttama māṉarū pāvatit taiyalē
       caraṇaṅkaḷ
       paṉṉaru niṉṉalap paravaiyi lēkaḷi
       tuṉṉi yiruntiyāṉ tuḷaitalum kūṭumō (uṉṉai)
       āṉanta valliyē yaṉpuṭa ṉēniṉ kaṇ
       māṉenta nāḷiṉil varavumeṉ māmati (uṉṉai)
       cuntaranāriyē tūyani ṉāṉaṉa
       cantira kāntiyi leṉmaṉa malaravē (uṉṉai)
       karantaṉai yāṉuniṉ kaiyiṉaip paṟṟiyē
       māntaḷir mēṉiyāy makiḻvatek kālamō (uṉṉai) (36)

   ō! īṇṭuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ yāvum nīyāyp pariṇamittut tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉavē! itō niṉ
   koṇṭalait taṇṭalaiyai veṉṟa pūṅkūntal tōṉṟukiṉṟatu! atōniṉ
   kūṉpiṟainutalum, kuḻiḻai nikar tuṇṭamum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa! āṇṭu avaḷ kūrmai
   taruvēlai vāḷai vaṇṭai makarattai nikar maiyeḻutu viḻikaḷ kāṇap
   paṭukiṉṟaṉavē!
   (pāṭukiṉṟāḷ)
